# Harborfall — Development Blueprint & Roadmap

*Port Sable is an original city and Harborfall an original game. This document
adapts the user's "complete open-world game blueprint" into Harborfall's
official roadmap. Every item is marked honestly against the shipped game:*

✅ **Done** · 🟡 **Partial** · ⬜ **Planned** (with target phase)

> **Official blueprint: user-supplied PDF, Nov 2026, 15 sections** — the PDF
> is the reference of record; this file reconciles it against the shipped
> game. Phase 4 (this release) shipped the PDF's cheap in-round items:
> crouch/stealth, phone Camera = photo mode, and shake/FOV settings.

---

## 1. World & Map
- ✅ Island harbour city (~250×250u), 6 named districts: The Docks, Market Row, Lantern Park, Gull Heights, Ferry Quarter, East Yards
- ✅ 61 buildings + 9 glass towers (34–54u), road grid, sidewalks, crosswalks, manholes, cycling traffic lights
- ✅ Landmark scenes: ferris wheel, beach + lifeguard hut, suburb houses + mansion, industrial port (cranes/containers/ships), airport scenery, highway gantry, nightclub exterior, countryside corner, "PORT SABLE" hillside sign
- ✅ Bay Bridge is now a DRIVABLE raised causeway (deck profile + ramps, ground-height sampling for player/cars) leading to **Coral Shores** — a second city across the bay: ~4×3 block grid, ~23 buildings + 4 glass towers with the facade treatment, palm boulevards, beach, fuel station #4, pawn shop, 4 parked cars, its own traffic loop (6 cars) + 6 peds, district name on HUD/minimap/big map. Police pursue across the bridge (Phase 4)
- ✅ Scenic hillside overlook with parked silver supercar (Phase 2)
- ✅ Safehouses — buy the Sable Heights Villa ($7,500): respawn moves there and its drive is a garage save point (Phase 3)
- ✅ Building interiors (PDF priority list) — safehouse ✅ (villa), garage ✅ (Lina's), club ✅ (Club Solace), police station ✅ (Harbour Patrol HQ lobby + desk surrender, Phase 7), clinic ✅ (Sable Clinic, heal inside, Phase 7), restaurant ✅ (Marina Table — sit + $25 set menu, Phase 7), airport hangar ✅ (Sable Field, Phase 9), gym ✅ (Sable Fitness — the gym moved indoors, Phase 14), second home ✅ (Northgate Loft — bed/wardrobe/harbour view, Phase 14), shop ✅ (Thread & Tide is a walk-in pocket interior — racks, counter, shop hours enforced at the door, Phase 15; register robbery points remain at 4 storefronts), farmhouse ✅ (the Downs farmhouse — kitchen/living + the free farmhand's bunk, Phase 16)
- ✅ Facade & street detail pass: window depth/blinds/lit variance, cornices, doors + canopies, wall AC, balconies, stepped annexes; grass tufts, park paths, wet sand, road arrows + stop bars, oil stains, rain puddles, hydrants/mail/bus stops/dumpsters/bicycles (Phase 3)
- ✅ Distance depth: far skyline silhouette ring + second bridge silhouette on the horizon (Phase 3)
- ✅ Further map expansion beyond Coral Shores — Northgate district + causeway + Sable Island (Phase 6); Sable Field working airfield + Sable Downs dirt loop (Phase 9)

## 3. Vehicles
- ✅ 11 drivable cars in 9 classes (sedan, hatch, coupe, pickup, van, supercar, muscle, SUV, classic) + rideable motorcycle + gold mansion supercar
- ✅ Steal any parked car; traffic cars react/flee
- ✅ Vehicle damage 0–100: engine smoke >60, power loss, wrecked at 100 (Phase 2)
- ✅ Fuel per car, HUD gauge, 3 fuel stations that refill for cash (Phase 2)
- ✅ Glossy paint with environment reflections, tread tyres, glowing lights; burnout/drift tyre smoke (Phase 2)
- ✅ Motorcycles — the beach Sable Stinger is rideable: seated rider, lean-in cornering, wheelie-lite launch, faster + tighter than cars, small tank (Phase 3; Phase 40: rider-synced lean, a held wheelie with a balance window + loop-out, and explicit census entries in BODY_DIMS / DRIFT_FACTOR)
- ✅ Garage car selection — stored-cars list (max 6, persisted) fed by parking in any garage zone (villa drive + Lina's bay); G opens the select UI with per-car speed/handling/damage/fuel cards; taking a car out swaps with the current one; original model names per kind (Meridian Sedan, Gullwing GT, …) (Phase 4)
- ✅ Vehicle-vs-vehicle physics collisions — player car vs traffic vs police: impulse bounce, speed exchange, damage to both, crash sfx + scaled camera shake; traffic brakes/honks (C++ `resolve_vehicle_collision` + TS mirror) (Phase 4)
- ✅ Vehicle interiors + animation — dashboard, rotating steering wheel (player car), seats/bench visible through clearer glass; front wheels steer on all cars; body pitch/roll from the C++ weight-transfer outputs (Phase 4 / Visual V4)
- ✅ Boats you can drive — speedboat (Phase 6) + fishing skiff, second class (Phase 9). Phase 28 handling pass: speed-sensitive helm (turn authority eases off up to 45% at full speed), planing bow rise + settle (presentation pitch), wake foam whose cadence/density follow the throttle, storm drift pushing the hull onshore (positional only — the fishing anchor test is speed-based and untouched), and a chart-edge clamp so a hull can no longer sail off the multiplayer server's world bounds. The Sparrow (Phase 9) gains the rest of the pass: a HUD STALL chip + visible nose-drop below flying speed (the Phase 9 stall sink already cost altitude; flight numbers unchanged), landing-gear compression on touchdown, and a prop blur disc keyed to engine speed · ✅ Vehicle customisation — Lina's mods: respray/engine/brakes/tyres (Phase 6). Phase 18 completes the tuning ladder: brakes gain tiers II–III (they shipped with one; bite scales 9/12/15 per second), engine tier III repriced to $1,800 at the top of its ladder, and DRIFT TYRES join as a toggle mod ($600: +35% drift score rate, −12% wet-asphalt grip on the step_vehicle_v3 surface path). Mod levels clamp to 0..3 on every save path (last-car, fleet, garage-stored) and driftTyres threads through all three. Phase 18 also adds two original cars: the SABLE KESTREL RS (track coupe, $18,000, dealer-only — the coupe shell track-trimmed: splitter, fixed wing, fat rear rubber, the sharpest handling in the range) and the BRONTO HAULER (heavy off-road pickup, $9,500, farm + dealer — capped for torque, and the one car whose grip improves on dirt; one ambient traffic unit on the farm-corner route wears the kind). Phase 28 adds two more originals on the same machinery: the VANTARA TEMPEST (flagship GT coupe, $26,000, dealer + one display-row car — a post-step top-end push to 26.5 u/s above the shared clamp, rear-drive with the joint-top drift factor 1.3) and the SABLE MULE (farm pickup, $3,200, dealer + parked at the farmstead — the best dirt grip of any pickup at ×1.38 on the surface path, road-capped at 11.5 u/s; NOT traffic: the traffic system is grid-bound with no district gating, so no Downs-only traffic is claimed)

## 4. Police & Wanted
- ✅ Wanted 1–5★, patrol cars spawn and pursue, BUSTED after 3s pinned at low speed
- ✅ Line-of-sight: cops only know your position while they (or the heli) see you (Phase 2)
- ✅ Last-known-position search: break sight → cops sweep the last seen area while heat cools (Phase 2)
- ✅ Witnesses: crimes near a pedestrian are "called in" after ~1.5s (watch for the **!**) (Phase 2)
- ✅ Roadblocks at 4+★: two patrol cars across your road ahead (Phase 2)
- ✅ Police helicopter at 4+★ with searchlight; red/blue light glow + real light cast from the nearest unit (Phase 2)
- ✅ 6th star / heavy response — heat rule at 5★ raises the 6th star; the response is the Harbour Guard SUV + K9 (Phase 9, see §15.6)
- ✅ Spike strips (Phase 6), police boats (Phase 9)
- ✅ Emergency-services dispatch simulation (PDF) — lite dispatch (Phase 15): incidents (collision clusters, prop fires, medical calls) generate on a timer at roadside spots; the nearest free Phase 7 ambulance/fire unit is dispatched and routes over the ROAD_COORDS grid with the traffic lane model (flashing beacon, dispatch barks, Harbour Wire notes, white-ringed red cross on both maps), works the scene ~8 s, clears and returns to circulation. Parked + traffic units and the rushing-ambulance world event date from Phase 7. Phase 16 audit hardening: a watchdog in the dispatch tick stands a call down if its unit is wrecked or its order is lost (Phase 15 could stall dispatch for the session in that case — the traffic loop skips wrecked cars before the dispatch branch)

## 10. Immersion
- ✅ Full day cycle (5 min), golden-hour opening, themes re-grade the whole city
- ✅ Dynamic weather: Clear / Cloudy / Rain / Storm / Fog, weighted state machine (Phase 2) — Phase 36: a committed FORECAST (the next state is rolled when the current one is set and shown on the phone's weather page + an imminent-turn HUD glyph), drying roads (road wetness outlives the rain by ~2.5 min and drives gloss/puddles/grip), targeted lightning (roof/sea strikes, spectacle only), weather chop on the water, storm turbulence on the Sparrow's pose, and windblown autumn leaves

- ✅ Storms: lightning bolt + sky flash + delayed thunder (Phase 2)
- ✅ Birds over the harbour, ambient helicopter, bobbing boats, club queue, streetlight halos (Phase 2) — Phase 42 grows the harbour's wildlife: a five-dolphin pod porpoising a scripted loop around Sable Island and the mainland shore (breach splash rings; a moving boat within 9u makes the nearest dolphins dive), and perched gulls on mooring posts at the pier, the island dock and the waterline that scatter when the player closes in and resettle when they leave. Scripted ambient life, honestly labelled — not an ecosystem simulation
- ✅ Loading theme song: 102 BPM funk/coastal groove with sections + menu ambience (waves, gulls) (Phase 2)
- ✅ Swimming — wade into deep water at the beach/harbour and paddle; shore exit (Phase 3)
- ✅ Environment motion — animated water surface (drifting normal map), palm crowns sway near the player (Phase 4 / Visual V5)

## 11. Multiplayer
- 🟡 WebSocket relay server (Python/FastAPI): rooms, state sync, remote avatars, anti-teleport validation — works when a server URL is configured; Vercel can't host the socket server (use Render/Railway/Fly/Docker). Phase 38 polish: server-sanitised display names (float over remote avatars, sign chat lines), room text chat (200-char cap, 1.5 s rate limit, validated + room-scoped), and room codes with a shared default room ("sable") — still 🟡 because presence is session-only: no accounts, no persistence of who was where, and a self-hosted server is required (see the Phase 38 note)
- ⬜ Co-op missions, shared wanted (Phase 6)

- ✅ Assists & accessibility — Drive assist (Off/Light/Full steering smoothing after the input merge, road vehicles only; Full adds gentle chase-camera auto-centre that yields to any orbit input; drift scoring reads the raw input and is untouched), Aim assist (Off/Soft/Strong magnetism of the free-aim shot direction — a fractional pull toward the nearest visible ped in a small cone, never lock-on, never through the blocker set; hip fire unassisted), Colourblind-safe HUD (one palette module, game/hudpalette.ts: Okabe–Ito swaps for the meaning-carrying HUD colours — minimap blips, wanted stars, heat chip, money figures, Bank event badges; HUD only, the world does not change), Reduced flash (lightning peak capped at its one write site; muzzle flash + impact sparks softened at theirs; the game has no explosion-flash effect to soften). All in Phone → Settings, every default = the pre-Phase-44 behaviour, persisted per save slot (Phase 44)
- ✅ Quality tiers — Low/Medium/High with first-run auto-detect + one-time FPS step-down; gates composer/SSAO/shadows/normals/CC0/water/paint (Phase 5)
- ✅ Real CC0 PBR textures — Poly Haven asphalt/concrete/brick/plaster embedded (955 KB, ASSETS_LICENSES.md); procedural route kept for Low + new plaster/tile/corrugated set (Phase 5)
- ✅ UI redesign + solidity — one design system, single right-side HUD stack, safe-area insets, compact mode <560px, zero selectable UI (Phase 5)
- ✅ Camera boom guard — third-person camera clamps against building AABBs instead of clipping inside them (Phase 5)
- ✅ Controller/gamepad support — standard mapping, analog sticks, triggers, LT free-aim (Phases 8–9)

## 13. Technical — Engine Note (honest)
Harborfall's engine is **Three.js / React Three Fiber + a C++17 physics core
compiled to WebAssembly (TypeScript fallback) + a Python FastAPI multiplayer
server**, packaged for desktop with Electron. One codebase ships everywhere:
web (Vercel), mobile browsers, Windows .exe and macOS .dmg. Rendering:
ACES filmic tone mapping, PCF-soft sun shadows that follow the player,
PMREM environment reflections, canvas-generated textures (zero asset files).
Unreal Engine 5 photorealism is **out of scope by design**: it cannot ship as
an instant-play browser game on free hosting, which is Harborfall's whole
distribution model. We chase "realistic-styled" — real proportions, textured
materials, filmic light — inside the web stack instead.
- ✅ Post-processing film grade: bloom, warm hue/contrast grade, film grain, vignette, SMAA — desktop only; auto-disabled on coarse-pointer/mobile devices (Phase 3)
- ✅ Procedural PBR maps — canvas height→normal maps (asphalt, concrete, brick, facade, sand, grass) + roughness variation on roads/sidewalks; deepened facade grime/AO (Phase 4 / Visual V2–V3)
- 🟡 Physics v2 — C++ source extended (vehicle step v2 grip/weight-transfer, vehicle collision, swept AABB, ped separation) with an exact TypeScript mirror; g++ smoke test passes. The shipped `.wasm` binary refresh still needs an Emscripten environment (emcc unavailable where this was built) — the loader falls back per-function, so behaviour is identical either way (Phase 4)
- ✅ LOD/perf — peds beyond 120u animate on a 3-frame tick; props/decals/tufts are single instanced draws (frustum-culled), so no district chunk toggles are needed (Phase 4 / Visual V7)

- ✅ **Phase 1** — the city, driving, police, story, themes, desktop/web builds (shipped)
- ✅ **Phase 2** — weather, police LOS/search + roadblocks + witnesses, damage & fuel, smartphone, NPC life, overlook + bay bridge, visual realism overhaul, character realism rebuild, poster loading screen, theme-song upgrade (this release)
- ✅ **Phase 3** — visual round 2 (rounded sculpted cars, post-processing grade, facade/ground/prop detail, skyline ring) + rideable motorcycle, two enterable interiors (garage + club), swimming, first-person camera, combat lite (punch + pistol), buyable villa with garage save (this release)
- ✅ **Phase 4** — UI realism overhaul (dark glass), garage car selection, character select (6 presets), C++ physics v2 + vehicle collisions, drivable Bay Bridge + Coral Shores (second city), theft system (pickpocket/robberies/break-ins/pawn), gyro steering, PDF deltas (crouch, photo mode, shake/FOV settings), Visual Upgrade V1–V7 (audit doc, normal maps, weathering, vehicle interiors, water/palm motion, debrief screen + weapon selector, far-ped tick skip) (this release)
- ✅ **Phase 4.5** — traffic obeys lights ✅ and rush hour ✅ landed in Phase 6; races + taxi jobs ✅ landed in Phase 7; gamepad + tow jobs ✅ landed in Phase 8 — closed
- ✅ **Phase 5 (shipped as the realistic-visuals + story/UI round)** — quality tiers, equirect env + water shader + SSAO, CC0 PBR route, clearcoat cars + headlight beams, character material/animation pass, deepened story + 2 side arcs + Ch.5 choice, cinematic subtitles, UI redesign. Still open from the original Phase 5 list: more interiors, customisation, NPC memory, businesses, 6-star design review
- ✅ **Phase 6 — "Proper Game" (this release)** — Northgate district + causeway + Sable Island; traffic obeys lights + queues + rush hour + reckless driver; bus (route + stops) / taxi / bicycle / speedboat; NPC destinations, phone-peds, dog walker, vendor, rain shelter, night club drift; police spike strips, surrender, heli searchlight, disguise-lite; SMG + shotgun, 4-slot Q, recoil, muzzle light, cover; stamina, skills + phone Stats, vault, limp, food/hospital heals; vehicle mods (B1); random world events (B2); shop hours (B3); character realism pass 3 (B4); C++ physics v3 source + TS mirror + smoke test (B5, WASM binary not rebuilt — no emcc); full game-flow pipeline (splash → staged loading → spawn sweep → streaming → area cards → respawn screens). Detailed 500-list mapping in §15.
- ✅ **Phase 7 — "The 500 Sweep, Round 1" (this release)** — jobs system (taxi fares with hailing peds, tips + star ratings; Lina's delivery chain) in a phone Jobs app; 3 street races with AI rivals, checkpoint framework, positions, prizes + optional $100 bet; interiors round (Harbour Patrol HQ + desk surrender, Sable Clinic, Marina Table restaurant); buyable businesses (Wraps stall / Car Wash / Club Solace share) with a single daily income tick (06:00 rollover — sleeping triggers the same tick) + Coral Shores Apartment (second home, garage-save #2, sleep); THE HARBOUR HEIST two-stage chain (prep stealth bonus → strongroom drill → escape); mission checkpoints + retry-from-checkpoint (R), chapter replay at 40% (Story app), mission stats; phone News (Harbour Wire, generated from player actions) + SableSocial apps; GPS route polyline on the big map; world sweep — causeway toll booth, 3 speed cameras, destructible crates/barrels/fences (respawn next day), bench/chair sitting, vendor cooler drink, wind gusts (palms/boats/HUD), fish; emergency vehicles (parked + traffic ambulance/fire + rushing-ambulance event); player idle variations + swim dive entry. Detailed per-topic account in §15.
- ✅ **Phase 18 — "Vehicles & Racing Depth"** — opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 17 (four defects fixed: photo evidence could be farmed outside its ring — the arm now disarms on leaving; B2 world events started over Phase 17 events/races/dispatch and their expiry cleared ANY event's courtesy flag — both directions now honour the shared contract; the tail mission's checkpoint retry rebuilt the banked-stop set sequentially, crediting stops never confirmed — the real set is snapshotted and restored, and the courier rejoins the loop at the current stop; wire3's story 2★ heat was never cleared on completion — the delivered exposé now spends the tip). DRIFT SCORING with chained multipliers and clean-release banking; THE SABLE CUP 4-round championship (arm-then-race from Phone → Jobs; $2,500 + headline + gold Vantara S voucher); TWO NEW ORIGINALS (Sable Kestrel RS, Bronto Hauler); TIER-3 TUNING completed (brakes II–III, engine III repriced) + DRIFT TYRES. Detailed account in the Phase 18 note.
- ✅ **Phase 19 — "Economy & Empire"** — opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 18 (three defects fixed, the first fatal: the Sable Cup's round ids matched NO race in the game, so the championship could never be armed, recorded or won — the rounds now carry the real RaceDef ids, with a save migration; a failed bet-short race start silently ate a Cup arm — arms now convert only at real starts; garage take-out was the one tuning apply path without clampMods). A FOURTH BUSINESS (Sable Marina Rentals, $15,000, +$380/day) with a working RENTAL DOCK ($75 day-pass skiff); BUSINESS UPGRADES 1→3 (+35% takings per level) for all four; STAFF TRAITS LIVE (Sana Iqbal's night bonus, settled as a day/night split); SABLEBANK LOANS ($500/$1,500/$4,000, 8%/day capped at 2× principal, 10% morning auto-repay, wanted-3★ lockout). Detailed account in the Phase 19 note.

COMBAT DEPTH: cover-lite (the Phase 6 cover geometry + a firearm in hand = combat cover: IN COVER chip, deeper tuck, peek rule on the sight/bust maths — with the honest scope that this build's police arrest rather than shoot, so there is no NPC accuracy roll to modify and none was invented), the Kestrel Carbine (new weapon kind end-to-end: spec, held auto-fire, Sable Arms counter + ammo + two fittings in WMOD, Q-cycle, mirrors, its own model/voice/teal FX tint, one mansion-drive pickup, point-tested), and suppression-lite (near-miss/heard flee rolls with a stacking/decaying heat model through the ordinary fleeUntil channel; police pressure stays on the single addCrime → applyCrime → heat path, verified — suppression adds no crime of its own). THE SABLE SLIDE SERIES (game/slide.ts + components/SlideSeries.tsx): a 3-round drift championship mirroring the Sable Cup's structure — per-slot progress (harborfall-slide), sequential unlock, arm from Phone → Jobs, the arm converting at the course's amber start ring. Each round is a gated course (gates in order inside the clock) with a banked-points target — Docks Yard 500/120 s, Bay Infield 800/150 s, Northgate Interchange 1,000/120 s — and the Series never scores a slide itself: it counts banks off DRIFT.bankSeq, so every point on the board is a point the drift system paid, and a collision still loses the open chain before it can bank. Champion: $3,000 flat (the Cup's way), a Wire headline, the career title in Stats → Records, and SLIDE GOLD — a champion's swatch in Lina's EXISTING respray palette (career honour, survives a series reset; no new cosmetic system). NG+ keeps the Series like the Cup (career record) and stands a live run down. LOCAL BESTS BOARD: Phone → Stats gains a Records section gathering the best drift chain + lifetime banked, the Slide title + per-round bests, all five race bests (per-race best times already persist in jobStats.bestTimes — verified for street, boat and air finishes alike), the derby best/wins and the best catch. SERVER LEADERBOARD: race bests fit the service's {race_best, job_earned} schema and already flow via postRaceResult; drift points do NOT fit (a higher-wins score is not a lower-wins time) and are deliberately not submitted — the contract is untouched. TALLY unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15.10 activities ✅ line grows).
- ✅ **Phase 35 — "Phone Media"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 34 (one defect fixed: a drift chain OPENED before a Slide Series GO — built during the countdown or in free roam — banked inside the run's first second and counted in full; the component now calls resetDrift() at GO, so only chains started inside the run can bank, and banks before GO were already excluded by the sequence snapshot. Decided + documented in game/slide.ts: a chain started after GO while driving the course counts wherever it banks — intended, drift.ts is the only scorer. The rest clean, verified in code: collision loses the open chain inside tickDrift before it can bank; bust/wasted stands a run down off the G.status edge and NG+ stands a live run down while the Series persists as a career record; the Records screen reads only real per-slot persisted sources (jobStats, slide, cup, HEAT) with the Sparrow par labelled; per-race bests are written by BOTH race finish blocks (street incl. Cup rounds, boat/air incl. the Bay Circuit Cup round) before recordCupResult; postRaceResult cannot throw into gameplay (the post is async with its own try/catch, the sync path length-guards its Math.min). On the gold-swatch/slot-wipe question: the game has NO slot-wipe feature anywhere — slots switch, they are never erased — and the slide key is slot-prefixed, so the gold is per-slot and a fresh slot starts clean; there is no wipe path to clear it through, stated plainly). SABLE TUNES (game/tunes.ts): the phone's Music app becomes a real music player — four original WebAudio compositions as selectable tracks: "Port Sable Sunset" (the opening theme) and "Club Solace" (the club's 116 BPM loop) from game/audio.ts, plus TWO NEW loops composed this phase — "Daylight Causeway" (96 BPM daytime cruise groove, Cmaj7|Am9|Fmaj7|Gadd9) and "Neon Meridian" (84 BPM night-drive synth, Am|F|C|G) — on the theme's lookahead-scheduler idiom through a shared music bus. Play/pause, prev/next, a track list; the readouts are honest (elapsed since the track was started + position inside the fixed loop — a loop has no total length, so there is no fake progress-to-end bar). CLUB PRIORITY: Interiors now routes the room transition through tunesClubEnter/tunesClubExit — entering Club Solace suspends the phone's track (unless it IS the club loop, which simply keeps playing across the door) and the room loop takes over; leaving resumes the suspended track. The title screen's theme is untouched; on Play, App calls resumeTunes() after stopping the title theme so a saved "playing" track picks up. Selection + playing intent persist per slot (harborfall-tunes). VOLUME HONESTY: the game has no separate volume setting — mute (N) is the one audio switch; the Tunes bus re-syncs to it on every scheduler tick, the same treatment the theme and club masters get in toggleMute, and the player UI says so instead of faking a slider. PHOTO GALLERY: captures already rendered-on-capture (the PhotoCapture component renders a frame before toDataURL — the standard answer to the preserveDrawingBuffer problem, now documented at the capture site); the Gallery keep limit rises 10 → 12 with oldest-replaced stated in the app, every new capture is labelled with its district via districtAt (streamed districts included), and the app gains a full-screen viewer (tap a shot → district, date, delete, and a note that the phone copy is the 320 px thumbnail while the full-res PNG downloaded at capture). The 320 px cap is documented where it lives (game/phase9.ts): ~15–35 KB a shot, 12 shots well under 0.5 MB of the shared ~5 MB localStorage quota — full-res data URLs at 1–4 MB each would evict real saves. Pre-Phase-35 shots carry no district and show their date alone rather than a guessed label. TALLY unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15.11 smartphone ✅ lines grow).
- ✅ **Phase 36 — "Weather Depth"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 35 (one defect fixed: Sable Tunes' elapsed clock kept counting while the club held the track — suspended silence was reported as listening time, and a track merely ARMED inside the club inherited the previous track's age; the clock now freezes at the suspend moment and an armed track starts from zero. The rest clean, verified in code: the club suspend/resume machine in every combination — club track selected (the room's startClubBass call is timer-guarded, no double-start; on exit the same loop simply keeps playing, carried by the phone), another track playing (stopped + suspended + resumed on exit), nothing playing (room loop starts and stops with the room), leaving mid-suspend, pausing inside the club on either kind of track, and the title→Play resume (every engine start is timer-guarded — startMenuTheme, startClubBass and the tunes scheduler all refuse a second start). Mute: no fight — toggleMute hard-sets the theme/club masters, the tunes bus is written only by its own scheduler's syncMusicBus from the same muted flag, and no periodic writer touches another source's gain. Per-slot isolation: saveslots.ts installs the prefix layer before any game module loads (main.tsx keeps it first; slot switching is a stamped reload), so harborfall-tunes and harborfall-gallery load per slot like every save. Gallery: the 12-cap keeps newest-first and drops the tail (oldest); galleryAdd/galleryDelete wrap every storage write in try/catch, so a full localStorage costs the photo, never a crash — and the full-res download already happened. The district label is read from districtAt at the shutter moment: districtAt resolves the streamed districts from world.ts's registry bounds (data-side, not the chunk), so an Eastmere/Westbrook/Northreach shot labels correctly — with one honest condition: a district labels once its chunk has LOADED, which is also the first moment there is district geometry to photograph; a shot over an unloaded district's open water labels by the mainland rules, because in the rendered world that is what it is. Slide: resetDrift at GO verified — it zeroes score/mult/open/active/angle/chainT/cleanT and touches neither bankSeq nor lastBank, so pre-GO banks stay excluded by the snapshot; a chain still OPEN at GO is killed with the slate — intended and documented in game/slide.ts since Phase 35: only chains started inside the run may bank into it). THE FORECAST (game/weather.ts): the machine used to roll its next state at the moment the old one expired — there was no next to show. Now pickKind() (the same seasonal weighted roll, extracted unchanged, summer-afternoon storm bonus included) is rolled when the current state is SET and held in FORECAST; expiry advances into it and re-rolls. setWeather re-rolls on every call, so a Y-key manual pick — the one thing that can rewrite the future — instantly earns a fresh forecast and the forecast is never stale. The pick may equal the current weather (the machine has always been able to roll "no change"); the phone reports that honestly as "Holding as …". harbourweather.ps gains a "Turning to" row beside its existing next-change timer, and the HUD weather chip gains an amber → glyph of the incoming weather in the last 10 game-minutes before the turn (a Port Sable day is 300 real seconds, so 10 game-minutes ≈ the final ~2 s — a last-moments warning; the full forecast lives on the phone). STORM CONSEQUENCES: lightning strikes have targets — ~45% land on the tallest roof within 150u, the rest on open ground at its real height or on the sea (y=0); the old bolt bottomed at y≈1.6 through roofs and under water alike. Spectacle only: no damage path exists or was added. Boats ride a weather chop (the swell multiplier grows up to ~×2.2 in a full storm — bob amplitude and pitch/roll worsen with the sea state; the Phase 28 storm drift is untouched). The Sparrow gets storm turbulence: two slow sine disturbances on the PRESENTATION pose only (±~0.05 rad) — car.pitch/car.roll and the ring gates never see it, so the Sparrow Run's 120 s par is exactly as reachable as in calm air. DRYING ROADS: G.roadWet soaks toward rainAmt in seconds and dries at 1/150 per second (~2.5 min soaked→dry); road gloss, puddle opacity, lamp halos and the wet-grip factor (formula untouched — its input is now the road's actual wetness) read roadWet, while rain particles, sky and water keep reading rainAmt. SEASON TOUCH: the audit confirms sunrise/sunset drift already exists (Phase 13's seasonSun warps the sun's arc and the daylight window by season) — not re-shipped as new. The one addition is AUTUMN LEAVES: a pooled 130-point field around the camera, amber/brown vertex colours from the autumn foliage palette, flutter-fall + sway + the Phase 7 wind value pushing them sideways, recycled upwind; the field fades in only while the season is Autumn and never shows indoors. TALLY unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15.1 dynamic-weather ✅ line grows).

And the NG+ stand-down's hand-rolled supply-run reset never cleared the Phase 42 day-serial stamp J.snagSerial — inert, but an exit path that leaves the stamp behind is the class Phase 42 hardened, so NG+ now routes through endSnagRun like every other exit. Everything else clean: the pod route never enters a streamed district's bounds and Wildlife is base-bundle with no chunk state; the pod hides inside pocket interiors while its position keeps updating for the fishing check; the dive rule reads only LOCAL G.cars — remote multiplayer players are separate scene objects, never in G.cars, so no phantom dives — and parked/anchored boats sit under the |speed| > 0.5 gate exactly as documented; the gull mooring posts are pass-through props, consistent with lamps and hydrants, which are likewise absent from isBlocked; the tow gate's jobBusy() swap verified complete). THE SABLE POSTCARDS (game/postcards.ts): eight challenges sold to The Wire's picture desk from Photo Mode — The Pod Breaching, Air Support (4★+), Storm over the Harbour, Golden Span, Breakwater Gold, Solace Neon, The Sign at Night, The Dome — each checked HONESTLY at the shutter press from what the capture pipeline knows (the real camera's position and facing, G.dayTime, G.weather, G.wanted, the pod's live per-member positions via the new POD_MEMBERS state), with the actual check printed next to every postcard in the Gallery app: there is NO image recognition and the app says so. Fees $100–$150 pay once each, the winning shot is stamped in the Gallery, progress persists per slot and survives NG+ like the Slide record, and the framed prints fill the Postcard Wall on the Northgate Loft's east wall (plaque at the full set, certificate in the phone, a Wire headline). Detection lives entirely apart from the mission photo machinery (photoArm / G.lastPhotoAt untouched). Audit section 12 pins every fixed anchor to the real world data it names. TALLY unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §8 Camera ✅ line grows).
- ✅ **Phase 44 — "Assists & Accessibility"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 43 (ZERO defects: a double shutter press inside one frame collapses into PhotoCapture's single guarded pass, so a press can never bank twice; the overlap rule holds in evaluatePostcardShot — first unsold match in desk order, one per press; a stamped Gallery photo can be evicted by the 12-cap while the postcard stays sold, because the book and the Loft wall read POSTCARDS.done, never gallery presence; POD_MEMBERS is five preallocated objects mutated in place — the publication allocates nothing per frame; the Postcard Wall's window listener is removed on unmount; NG+ never touches the postcards key or module state, so the set survives the reset; the Phase 43 Part A fixes re-verified at their sites — rollCatch reads the live F.castX/F.castZ, NG+ routes its supply-run stand-down through endSnagRun). FOUR NEW SETTINGS in Phone → Settings → Assists & accessibility (game/store.ts settings, per-slot via the merge, every default = current behaviour): DRIVE ASSIST (Off/Light/Full) smooths the merged steering input — it sits after the keys/gamepad/touch/gyro merge in Vehicle's stepDrive, so every input source is assisted identically; the smoothed value chases the raw one with a speed-growing time constant (Light ~0.05 s parked → ~0.18 s at pace, Full ~0.08 s → ~0.30 s), road vehicles only (the boat helm and the plane keep raw input); no physics number, top speed or grip figure moves, the DRIFT FEED reads the RAW steering so scoring is bit-for-bit the old model, and the handbrake/wheelie inputs are read from the key set, never from steering. Full adds CAMERA AUTO-CENTRE: after ~2.5 s without an orbit input (OrbitControls 'start' — mouse/touch/wheel — or the gamepad right stick, both stamped), the chase camera's azimuth eases toward behind-the-car at ≤0.55 rad/s through the same spherical-offset mechanism as the pad orbit; pitch/distance untouched, aim/FP/photo/menu cameras never reach it. AIM ASSIST (Off/Soft/Strong) is MAGNETISM at Combat's fire() resolution site: the model is hitscan along a direction with a perpendicular hit corridor, so while FREE-AIMING the shot direction is pulled part-way (Soft ~5.7° cone / 45% pull, Strong ~11° / 70%) toward the nearest standing ped in the cone whose segment is clear of the isBlocked AABB set — the same blockers the cover system and camera guard read. Not lock-on: no snap, no tracking, no through-wall help; hip fire follows the character's facing and is never assisted. COLOURBLIND-SAFE HUD (Off/On): ONE new module (game/hudpalette.ts) owns every meaning-carrying HUD colour — the minimap/big-map canvas reads it for police/objective/job/your-car/player/traffic/incident blips, the DOM reads it for wanted stars, the heat chip, money figures (Bank +$/day and rent, the loan owed, the debrief reward) and the Bank's business-event badges (+50%/−30%/fine/passed). Off = today's colours exactly (canvas) and no inline styles (DOM); On = the Okabe–Ito palette (police sky blue — the stars take the same blue; targets yellow; you and your cars white; traffic orange; incident vermillion; money gain bluish-green / loss vermillion, always alongside the +/− signs). HUD only — the world's teal/amber art does not change, and the setting's help text says so. (Honest note: standard-mode money was already sign-carried text with no green/red coding; the swap ADDS the safe colours in CB mode rather than replacing an unsafe pair.) REDUCED FLASH (Off/On) softens the real flash sites: the lightning strike's peak is capped at its ONE write site in Weather (G.lightning 1 → 0.3), which scales every consumer — bolt opacity, GameSystems' ambient bump, sky/fog brightening — and shortens the flash under the fixed decay; Combat's muzzle-flash sprite burns dimmer and smaller, its pooled light caps at 7 instead of 26, and the impact sparks fade. There is NO explosion-flash effect anywhere in the game (verified by census), so none was invented to soften. SUBTITLE SIZE needed no work: it already exists (Phase 5 — S/M/L in Phone → Settings and the pause menu, CSS scaling both the dialogue renderer, 13/15/20 px, and the bark renderer, 11/13/15 px); verified, not re-claimed. TALLY: 87 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ over 94 topic lines — ONE NEW ✅ line (§12 Assists & accessibility); no existing line changed state.
- ✅ **Phase 45 — "Performance Round 2"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 44 (ONE defect fixed: Drive Assist Full's camera auto-centre ran for boats and the plane too, contradicting the documented road-vehicles-only scope — behind the plane it actively fought the player, chasing the tail through every banked turn; it now carries the same boat/skiff/plane exclusion as the steering smoothing. Everything else clean: the smoothed steer resets on drive start and tracks raw input whenever the assist is off or out of scope, so no stale value can carry into the next car; the aim/FP/photo/interior/menu cameras all return or gate before the auto-centre block, and the tram/bus rides never set G.driving so they can't reach it either; aim magnetism runs only inside fire() while G.aiming, over the bounded ped pool with ~1.2u segment sampling — per-shot cost, never per-frame; the colourblind palettes' Off path returns the shipped canvas colours and null DOM accents, and every caller passes `undefined` styles for nulls, so there is no layout shift; every G.lightning consumer is multiplicative (bolt opacity, ambient bump, sky/fog brightening), so the reduced-flash 0.3 peak scales the spectacle proportionally and nothing thresholds or divides on a 1.0 peak; old saves pick up all four Phase 44 settings through the settings spread-merge). PART B — MEASURED FIRST, THEN ACTED ON EVIDENCE: the poster key-art panels (~318 kB of base64 JPEG, start-screen only) were statically imported into the initial bundle; they now load through a dynamic import as their own chunk behind the splash screen — INITIAL LOAD FALLS 866.2 → 629.0 kB gzip (−27%). The LOW TIER becomes a real weak-phone preset: shadows OFF entirely (the shadow pass is the largest single GPU cost a weak phone can shed), DPR cap 1.25 → 1.0, ambient traffic/crowd density capped at the Low lever values whatever the density levers say, and harbour wildlife updates at half rate — the composer was already fully off at Low, so there was no pass left to remove. Honest negatives, verified in code and left alone: the fixed-step sim's allocations are event-driven, not per-tick churn (the depenetration sweep's array work runs at 1 Hz); canvas textures are already ≤512 (faces 128); the one oversized texture (the 2048×320 hillside sign) is a single city-wide landmark, kept. No FPS is claimed — the HUD counter on the player's own phone is the verdict. TALLY unchanged: 87 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15 Smoothness line grows).
- ✅ **Phase 47 — "Two More World Events"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 46 (ZERO defects: a user-paused Tunes track leaves playing=false and roomHold untouched — outside a room the ambient score returns exactly as the priority chain intends, inside a room the hold keeps the score gated, and no state exists where the score and a held track sound together; the tension latch is written only at bar lines in the scheduler, so a mid-bar wanted drop holds the minor side to the bar's end; scoreStart is a one-way guard called once at Play — NG+ never calls it, so no scheduler restart or interval leak; the ambientScore default reaches old saves through the settings spread-merge; the score re-syncs its musicBus gain every 90 ms tick, so the N mute lands like Tunes'). PART B — TWO DAY EVENTS (new game/dayevents.ts + components/DayEvents.tsx), joining the world's event discipline at DAY scope beside the Phase 41 business events: one roll per day inside advanceDay's serial guard (~30% Harbour Regatta / ~30% Coral Street Market / ~40% quiet — one slot, so the two can never share a day), persisted per slot under harborfall-dayevents with the serial-inert read rule, announced at the roll by a Harbour Wire headline + a phone message. THE REGATTA: on its day a buoy course stands in the bay (teal start gate, six amber checkpoint buoys on the Phase 42 pod waters); crossing the gate in any boat arms a timed run — the Slide Series' sibling-module pattern (the Cup's J.race demands a ghost rival, and the regatta is honestly a TIME TRIAL against the clock — no AI racers exist and none are faked); checkpoint sequence + live clock, best time into Phone → Stats → Records as "⛵ Harbour Regatta" through the records pattern (+ a server best-time submission), and a $250 purse for beating 1:00, once per regatta day. THE MARKET: on its day three canopy stalls + a small crowd stand on the Coral Shores promenade (audit-sited: clear of the driven lanes, the bridge ramp landing and every promenade door) — a food stall on the Harbour Wraps food rules, a parts stall whose day's deal (one of engine tune / brakes / drift tyres / respray, rolled with the day) is a 25%-off voucher spent inside Lina's EXISTING bench — one price function (marketPrice) feeds the bench's display and its charge, and purchases are instant, so a voucher can never strand mid-transaction — and a curios stall that fences your stash at +20% through the existing fence pricing with a bonus flag the stall re-checks at the moment of sale. Both events despawn at day end; a live regatta run stands down unsettled on bust/wasted, day turn or NG+ (the day record itself carries across NG+, like the business events). Audit section 13 pins the course + stalls; headless check 321/321. TALLY unchanged: 87 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15 Random world events line grows 8 → 10).
- ✅ **Phase 48 — "The Harbour Crown" (endgame empire chain)** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 47 (ONE defect fixed + one gate hardened: ModMenu's buy() charged from the render closure's market-deal value — the panel re-renders on a 400 ms tick, so across a midnight boundary a click could charge the old day's voucher price and, on back-to-back market days, consumeMarketDeal could eat the NEW day's voucher; the click now re-reads marketDealActive() live, so the charge is always the live price (the displayed price can lag ≤400 ms, the charged price never does). And the regatta's arm gate claimed the Phase 17 courtesy list but omitted two of its tokens — it now also stands off for a live dialogue and a live dispatch incident. The rest clean, verified in code: the day roll fires exactly once on both advanceDay entry paths (the serial guard + the roll's own serial check + the Phase 31 SLEEP_TICK); every regatta exit releases G.eventNow (finish, the bust/wasted status edge, day-turn stand-down, the >5 s out-of-boat abandon, NG+); sellStash's market bonus is re-read at the moment of sale; NG+ leaves no buoy/stall state (the meshes render from the serial-keyed day record, which carries across NG+ by design); the score gate and the poster import's catch are intact. Two notes, not defects: a regatta run live at a day turn into a second regatta day survives gracefully onto the new day's record (a DayEvents comment says the run "goes with" the day — comment/code mismatch only), and a mission can start mid-regatta-run because mission starts never consulted G.eventNow — the pre-existing Phase 17 courtesy pattern, unchanged). THE HARBOUR CROWN (side key "crown", four parts, Ines Marlo): the post-story finale for players who built an empire — gated on Ch.6 done AND ≥2 businesses AND ≥1 rental, the gate stated with the player's own counts in Ines's locked dialogue (the Phone → Story row shows the same counts). crown1 "Paper Trail" works the ledger engine fields across two districts (a meet step at Westbrook's Breakwater Point, two photo steps in Eastmere, then the runTimer run to the newsroom — the engine's tail kind and photo kind are mutually exclusive in one def, so the moving-van tail was NOT duplicated; the shadowing is meet-then-photograph, voss2-style). crown2 "Cold Storage" copies a container manifest on an 8 s hold-channel (the heist2 drill pattern) whose seal alarm trips 2★, under a mission wanted CEILING of 3★ — a per-frame clamp inside the mission block, Voss's port-authority pressure as narrative, no new police system — then the standard escape. crown3 "The Auction" is the Ch.5-pattern choice pair in its OWN persisted flags (harborfall-crownchoice/crowndone/crownpaid): BUY OUT wires $40,000 to escrow AT DECISION TIME (only offered when the player has it — with less in hand the choice panel carries EXPOSE alone after Ines says why; non-refundable; a retry can never double-charge) and at completion every business pays a permanent +10% harbour dividend INSIDE businessDailyNet, so the Bank's figure and the payout are the same number; EXPOSE pays the $15,000 whistleblower reward through payoutAlt, raises reputation in all 14 districts, and prints a THREE-EDITION Wire series (edition 1 at completion; editions 2–3 on the next two morning ticks from game/crown.ts, inside advanceDay's serial guard). crown4 "Harbour Crown" runs the deeds by boat up the east channel to the observatory's cliff jetty (open water east of the Northreach land bounds — audit §14 walks the sailing line) and the receipt by car to Voss, at mission-set 4★ (cleared on delivery, the wire3 precedent). REWARD: the VANTARA SOVEREIGN — a new car kind through the full Phase 28 census (CAR_MODELS 268/91, honestly under the hyper's 288/92; CarBody body with a fixed two-tone livery — the car's teal paint under gold roof/spear/band; Vehicle BODY_DIMS + push-club cap 28.5; DRIFT_FACTOR 1.3; depenetrate HALF_W; both kind unions) — never sold, never spawned, never traffic; delivered by game/crown.ts through the garage machinery (storedCars, or the villa garage-save when the list is full), recorded in M.crownCarDelivered with existence checks, so it survives reload and NG+ (cars are kept; the arc's right to re-earn resets, a duplicate can never mint). Plus the auction set-piece (components/CrownWorld.tsx — auctioneer + two bidders at the mansion frontage while Part 3 is live) and the 👑 Harbour Crown title in Phone → Stats. NG+ resets the Crown with the other arcs (dividend ends with crownDone). Audit section 14 pins all four defs' shapes, every target, the sailing line and the Sovereign census. - ✅ **Phase 46 — "Dynamic Music"** — a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 45 (ONE defect fixed: the rush-hour reserve's park/unpark pass in GameSystems read the traffic-density lever directly, bypassing game/perf.ts — the Phase 45 Low ceiling capped the active-car count correctly (the density gate dormants beyond-share cars earlier in the same step, verified), but on Low with the lever on High the reserve cars inside the share never parked off-peak, against the preset's "ceiling whatever the levers say" rule; the decision now goes through a new perf.ts helper, trafficReserveAlwaysOut(), and Medium/High behaviour is byte-identical. Everything else clean: a failed poster-chunk import is caught at the mount effect, the cached rejected promise means no retry loop, the slideshow and caption guard on an empty panel set, and the Play path never reads poster state; the sun's castShadow is rewritten every frame, so a mid-session tier change re-applies on the next frame with no flicker, and the light remounts on its quality key besides; Wildlife's half rate cannot double-step the porpoise cycle — the phase is a pure function of the clock and everything else integrates the summed dt; the Phase 45 auto-centre exclusion is in place and the tram/bus rides never set G.driving, so they can't reach the block). PART B — THE AMBIENT SCORE (new game/score.ts): an original procedural free-roam score on the house lookahead-scheduler idiom — a pad bed in the opening theme's own chord family (Fmaj7 | Dm9 | Bbmaj7 | Cmaj7) at an 84 BPM ambient grid, a sparse pluck layer whose gain follows G.nightFactor (in across dusk, out at dawn; days are pad-only), and a wanted pulse (low thump on the beats at 3★+, doubling to 8ths at 5★+) with the pad progression shifting at bar lines to the relative-minor side (Dm9 | Bbmaj7 | Gm9 | Am7) while wanted ≥3★. All layer fades are ~2 s ramps — nothing hard-cuts. PRIORITY, as a citizen of the existing discipline rather than a parallel system: a playing Sable Tunes track outranks it, a room hold (Club Solace / diner jukebox — the single roomHold slot, read through a new tunesRoomHold() export) outranks it, then the score, then silence; the gate is re-evaluated every scheduler tick from the shared state, so no transition calls into the score. It starts only at the Play transition (scoreStart() in beginPlay — the start screen stays the theme's), rides the shared musicBus so the N mute lands within ~90 ms like Tunes, and missions have no dedicated music in this game (only sfx stings), so the score simply continues under them. Settings → Music → Ambient score (default On; Off = Phase 45 audio behaviour exactly). HONESTY: all synthesis, no samples; it is layered loops keyed to game state, not "adaptive AI music"; cost is 4 persistent gain nodes + one 90 ms interval, and while gated it schedules nothing. Initial load ≈629.9 kB gzip (Phase 45's 629.0 + ~0.9 kB of audio code); the poster split is untouched. TALLY unchanged: 87 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ (the §15 Sable Tunes line grows).

Detailed per-topic account in §15.

- ✅ **Phase 14 — "Depth Pass" (this release)** — VERLET RAGDOLL: a real 13-point verlet skeleton in the C++ core (ragdoll_step + exact TS mirror + smoke tests) drives downed peds near the player and the WASTED collapse, seeded from impact momentum, blended out on get-up (the ragdoll line goes ✅); DESTRUCTIBLES ROUND 2: fences/stalls/signs + café & showroom glass join the shared day-persistent breakage set (43 props + 15 panes, car + bat + bottle); TWO MORE INTERIORS (pockets 9+10): SABLE FITNESS (the gym moves indoors — same stations, prices and once-a-day rules; the park corner is stretching mats now) and the NORTHGATE LOFT ($9,000 second home: bed, wardrobe for owned outfits, harbour-view windows; owning both properties works); TWO NEW ACTIVITIES on the existing race scaffolding: the BAY CIRCUIT boat race (marina → around Sable Island → home, one ghost rival paced by race_pace, $500 win purse) and the SPARROW RUN plane time-trial (altitude-gated rings, landing finish, $600 under-par purse), both with phone Jobs entries + best times; SAVE SLOTS: three profiles via a localStorage prefix layer with a copy → verify → keep-the-originals migration of pre-Phase-14 saves into Slot 1, slot select on the start screen; KEY REMAPPING for interact/sprint/jump/weapon/phone/map with conflict detection, per slot; and a HIGH-TIER STABILITY PASS (same release, from a player crash report): capped DPR (High 1.5), cheaper SSR that never runs with SSAO, a hard-capped planar-water target, an error boundary + WebGL context-loss recovery, and reflections defaulting OFF (one-time migration) — see VISUAL_AUDIT Phase 14. Detailed per-topic account in §15.

- ✅ **Phase 16 — "World Depth & Player Control" (this release)** — opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 15 (three defects fixed: a wrecked dispatched unit stalled dispatch for the whole session — now a watchdog stands the call down; a failed/abandoned Meridian Pair attempt locked both branches forever — completion is now recorded and the unfinished branch is re-offered by its own giver; the farm south spur's comment cited a nonexistent road and a house clipped its edge — spur re-sited and verified clear against the generated world). PLAYER PERFORMANCE LEVERS: Traffic density / Pedestrian density / View distance in Pause → Settings and Phone → Settings, persisted per slot, live without reload (Low traffic runs half the ambient pool — buses, taxis, emergency units exempt, nothing culled within 25u; High keeps the rush reserve out all day; view distance scales streaming radii + fog; Normal = the pre-Phase-16 behaviour exactly). ARREST ESCORT: a filled bust meter on foot now plays as a scene — the nearest patrol officer walks to you, you walk hands-up beside them to the patrol car (≤12 s), and the standard BUSTED flow lands unchanged; surrender stays instant. FARM LIFE: the Downs farmhouse becomes interior #12 (kitchen/living + the farmhand's bunk — free sleep that does NOT move your respawn home, by design) and FARM WORK joins the phone Jobs app (3 crate loads, farm pickup → Harbour Wraps, $120/load + $150 bonus, crates visibly stack in the bed). PARKED FLEET: up to 3 owned cars persist where you park them (harborfall-fleet, same clearing rules as last-car, numbered amber rings on the big map; a pre-Phase-16 owned last-car migrates to fleet entry #1). BARBER VARIETY: 4 new Sable Cuts styles (fade, quiff, mohawk, shoulder length) + beard service (clean / stubble / goatee / full, $15) on the LOOK layer. Detailed per-topic account in §15.

**Status tally (recounted Phase 16):** the §15 ledger below is the per-topic account of the user's 500-feature list — **93 topic lines: ✅ 86 · 🟡 7 · ⬜ 0**. The ledger is fully closed: every topic is either implemented or carries a named lightweight equivalent. Phase 16 changed no line states — five 🟡 lines grew (arrest, countryside, enterable buildings, persistent world, faces/hair). Blueprint sections §§1–13 above remain the design roadmap.
*QA note (PDF §QA): each release is accepted against build-pass + in-game smoke checks of the new systems; a formal written QA checklist pass is tracked as a Phase 5 item.*

## 15. The 500-feature list — mapped (Phase 14, "Depth Pass")

The user's expansion brief listed ~500 desired features in 12
categories. This is the honest ledger, rewritten topic-by-topic in
Phase 7: ✅ implemented and playable · 🟡 lightweight equivalent (the
equivalent is named) · ⬜ not in — with the specific reason (engine
limit of the browser build, or not yet built). Nothing is marked done
that isn't playable, and nothing is silently skipped.

**Tally: 112 topic lines — ✅ 104 · 🟡 8 · ⬜ 0.** (Recounted line by line in Phase 50 — every state-marked line in §15 re-read; the recount corrects the running tally, which had folded growth lines into parent topics and under-counted the 🟡 lines by one — see the Phase 50 note. No line changed state in the recount; only the count is now honest.)

**Languages note (Phase 8):** the authorship of game logic was rebalanced toward the compiled/service languages as far as the platform allows — the **Harborfall Core (C++)** now owns traffic decisions, pedestrian steering+separation, wanted LOS sampling, boat buoyancy and race pacing (with g++ smoke tests and exact TypeScript mirrors; the WASM binary itself is rebuilt only where emcc exists); the **Python** FastAPI service adds leaderboards + economy snapshots; **C#** covers desktop world/save tooling (`tools/`). TypeScript remains the browser presentation shell + glue — browsers execute JS/WASM natively, which is a platform constraint, not a preference (see README "Languages").

### 1. Open World & Map
- ✅ Three connected landmasses: Port Sable (6 districts), Coral Shores, Northgate, plus Sable Island
- ✅ Eastmere (Phase 25): a compact harbour district east of Northgate across its own causeway — 9 buildings + a diner + the Eastmere Light lighthouse, 3 streets, the Gull Rock Lookout viewpoint — shipped as a streamed lazy chunk (see the Phase 25 note for the architecture), traffic-free with parked cars + ambient peds
- ✅ Westbrook (Phase 26): the second streamed district — a harbour-industrial shore west of the Ferry Quarter across the z=20 causeway, 8 buildings + a three-tube grain silo + 2 dock cranes, 2 streets, the Breakwater Point viewpoint — its own streamed lazy chunk on the generalised loader (streamed districts: 2), traffic-free with parked cars + ambient peds
- ✅ Northreach (Phase 31): the third streamed district — a quiet clifftop headland north of Northgate across a short bridge continuing the x=−20 road column, 6 cottages/tea house/store + a stone chapel + an observatory dome, 2 streets, the Northreach Point viewpoint — its own streamed lazy chunk on the same loader (streamed districts: 3), traffic-free with parked cars + ambient peds
- ✅ Bay Bridge + causeway crossings, fully drivable, police pursue across
- ✅ Drivable 2-deck parking garage (Northgate)
- ✅ Interiors I: Lina's Garage + Club Solace pocket rooms (Phase 3)
- ✅ Interiors II (Phase 7): Harbour Patrol HQ lobby (front desk = surrender point, wanted-poster wall, cells), Sable Clinic (waiting room, heal $50 inside), Marina Table restaurant (sit at tables, $25 set menu → full heal + stamina boost)
- ✅ Interiors III (Phase 13): Sable Motors showroom (exotics on rotating turntables, dealer counter indoors, glass front onto the forecourt) + Café Coral (Docks strip — coffee $8/stamina, harbour pastry $12/+35 health, sit-able tables, two resident regulars)
- ✅ Minimap + big map over all districts, district title cards, GPS route (see §11)
- ✅ Landmarks: ferris wheel, lighthouse, port cranes/ships, airport scenery, PORT SABLE hillside sign
- 🟡 Enterable buildings generally — equivalent: 14 hand-built pocket-room interiors (Phase 23 note: the count stays 12 — Club Solace's room was already one of them since Phase 3; Phase 23 makes it a WORKING nightclub rather than adding a room: doors 20:00–04:00, a bouncer who refuses wanted 3★+, a working bar, a colour-cycling floor grid, 7 dancers and a bass loop. Phase 9 adds the Sable Field hangar; Phase 13 adds the Sable Motors showroom — turntables + a working dealer counter behind a glass forecourt front — and Café Coral on the Docks strip, with a coffee/pastry counter, sit-able tables and resident regulars; Phase 14 adds SABLE FITNESS — the gym indoors, bench + treadmill with the same $10/once-a-day rules — and the NORTHGATE LOFT, a second purchasable home with bed, wardrobe and harbour-view windows; Phase 15 adds THREAD & TIDE, a walk-in clothing shop — racks + counter indoors, the street rack is signage, hours enforced at the door; Phase 16 adds the SABLE DOWNS FARMHOUSE — kitchen/living room + the farmhand's bunk, free sleep that deliberately does not move your respawn home; Phase 39 adds two rooms in the STREAMED districts: the EASTMERE DINER — Petra behind her own counter, a 3-item menu on the café/restaurant food rules, booths, a jukebox running Daylight Causeway on the Sable Tunes room-hold machinery, doors 06:00–23:00 — and the WESTBROOK SILO OFFICE, a lookout room up the grain silo (the door fade is the lift, stated on the sign) whose desk holds the harbour log: the live Wire headlines + district reputation off the real stores. Both pockets ride the base bundle with every other interior — the registry is one table; the districts' street geometry stays lazy, and each door is gated on its district's chunk being loaded, which is also the only state in which the player can stand at it) + open parking decks; full per-building interiors are a scope limit (each room is hand-built), not an engine limit
- 🟡 Countryside / playable airport — equivalent: Sable Field is a WORKING airfield now (hangar interior, flyable Sparrow on the strip, tower + terminal blockers) and the countryside corner is now a small farm network (Phase 15): the Sable Downs dirt loop grows three connected dirt roads (west/north/south — dirt grip via the step_vehicle_v3 surface path, drawn on both maps) to a farmstead — barn + farmhouse (exterior + blockers), an instanced crop field and a farm pickup spawn. Phase 16 makes the corner a place you WORK: the farmhouse is walk-in (interior #12, free farmhand bunk) and FARM WORK runs from the barn — load crop crates onto the farm pickup, haul 3 loads to Harbour Wraps ($120/load + $150 shift bonus, crates stack visibly in the bed), phone Jobs entry + stats. It is still a corner network, not a rural region — the streaming/draw budget is spent on the three districts, so the line stays 🟡
- 🟡 Destructible buildings — equivalent (Phase 14 round 2): 15 breakable glass panes (Coral row + the Café Coral windows + the Sable Motors showroom front) and 43 destructible props (crates, barrels, wooden fences in the suburbs/Sable Downs/mansion row, Market Row stall frames + canopies, road signs, the park fence line) smash from car rams, the bat and thrown bottles through one shared breakage state (game/destruct.ts), with debris from the shared debris_step; the Lantern Park scaffold topples when rammed; all persist for the game day and are repaired overnight. The buildings themselves stay static merged geometry — no structural destruction solver exists in the browser physics mirror

### 2. Player & Character
- ✅ 6 character presets × 3 outfit variants (character select)
- ✅ Crouch/stealth + cover, vaulting, limp, stamina + 4 skills with perks (Strength added Phase 8 via the gym)
- ✅ Swimming — plus dive entry (Phase 7: Space at a deep-water edge arcs you in head-first)
- ✅ Sitting (Phase 7: benches city-wide + restaurant chairs; E or any movement key stands)
- ✅ Idle life (Phase 7: after 6 s idle — weight shift, look-around, periodic phone check)
- ✅ Eating/drinking poses (vendor wrap, cooler drink, restaurant meal — hand-to-mouth)
- ✅ First-person camera + photo mode
- 🟡 Faces/hair — equivalent: Phase 8 realism pass 4 — 1024² painted faces on Medium/High (brow ridge, two-tone iris + catchlight, lid lines, stubble density), layered alpha hair cards over the scalp cap, subsurface-look skin, heel-toe stride-matched gait, breathing + blink. Phase 16 widens the barber: Sable Cuts offers 9 hairstyles (fade, quiff, mohawk and shoulder-length join the original five) plus a beard service — clean-shaven / stubble (painted face variant) / goatee / full beard (layered beard cards on the rig), $15 against the $25 cut, persisted on the LOOK layer per slot, reset on character change, and visible in the character-select preview. Still procedural rigs, realistic-STYLED, not scanned/photoreal (browser rig limit, stated since Phase 2)
- ✅ Clothing shop (Phase 8) + haircuts & tattoos (Phase 9): Thread & Tide on Market Row — 6 outfit pieces ($40–$200) applied as an override layer on the current preset (top style + top/bottom colours). Sable Cuts next door (barber): 5 hair styles (crop / flat top / bun / ponytail / bald) × 6 hair colours, $25, applied to the rig's hair system; Port Ink: 3 tattoo designs, $60, worn as an ink band on the forearm. Clothing, look (`harborfall-look`) — Character Select character/variant changes clear BOTH overrides (the documented change-out rule)
- ✅ Ragdoll (Phase 14) — a genuine VERLET point-mass ragdoll: a 13-point skeleton (pelvis, chest, head, shoulders/elbows/hands, knees/feet) with 18 distance constraints, gravity, terrain collision + friction, authored in the C++ core as ragdoll_step with an exact TypeScript mirror (g++ smoke-tested). It is seeded from the impact velocity of whatever downed the body (car momentum, bat, bullet, bottle), steps inside the fixed 60 Hz sim, and the humanoid rig's bones are posed from the solved points (mapping documented in game/ragdoll.ts); on recovery it blends back to the rig's get-up over ~0.35 s. Scope note: near-field only (≤ 40u) — far downed peds keep the static collapse, and the player's WASTED fall uses the same solver

### 3. Vehicles & Transportation
- ✅ 14 car classes + motorcycle + bicycle + bus + taxi + speedboat, damage/fuel, garages, mods, break-ins
- ✅ Exotic cars (Phase 8, all ORIGINAL designs — no real brands): Vantara S wedge supercar (scissor doors, Sable Motors $25,000, 1 rare traffic), Kestrel One hypercar (active rear wing, $40,000, mansion showpiece), Marlowe GT luxury tourer (dealership + traffic); plus the Sable Wrecker tow truck (see §10)
- ✅ The taxi is a working job vehicle (Phase 7 fare system)
- ✅ Emergency vehicles (Phase 7): ambulance + fire truck — parked units at the clinic/fire point, one traffic variant each on slow loops, lights-off in normal service
- ✅ Two garage-save homes: villa drive + Coral apartment parking (Phase 7)
- ✅ Boats (Phase 9): TWO classes — the Marina Racer speedboat (fast, planing) and the Harbour Fisher 180 skiff (slower workboat with canopy + crates; spawns at Sable Island and the Coral dock), both with wake + beaching; plus a police patrol boat under §6. No yacht ownership (no use case in the economy yet)
- ✅ Player-flyable plane (Phase 9): the Sable Sparrow at Sable Field — a full arcade flight model in the vehicle system: ground roll on the strip, lift-off past ~60 km/h, Shift climbs / Ctrl descends, stall sink below ~47 km/h, 60 u ceiling, building-strike checks while airborne, hard touchdowns and water ditches cost real damage, no bailing out mid-air. Helicopters stay AI-only (ambient + police) — no player heli model this round
- ✅ Trains (Phase 13) — **the Sable Line light-rail tram — the city-scale form of the topic; no heavy-rail network in Port Sable's plan.** One two-car tram (original design, teal/amber livery) runs an elevated guideway loop forever: Docks → Market Row → the causeway → Northgate → back across → the bay bridge → Coral Shores, with 4 stations (platforms, name boards, timetable boards, ~6 s dwells, door chimes). The guideway rides above the road centres on median pillars that are deliberately outside the collision set, and climbs the bridge ramps with the deck, so it never touches the traffic AI. Player ride mode: E to board while it dwells, sit in the front car, E to step off at any stop (stations only — no mid-route drops). Riding never bleeds wanted heat and the patrol never boards. The loop + live tram dot are on the map; Harbour Wire and SableSocial carry the launch flavour

### 4. Traffic & Road
- ✅ Signal obedience + queueing, rush-hour density, reckless driver, honking, bus route with stops — Phase 17: the bus is RIDEABLE (4 stops, $2 fare, seated riding, tram-identical wanted treatment, live amber map dot + stop markers)
- ✅ Toll booth (Phase 7): Coral causeway — $5 auto-charge through slowly, barrier raises; smashing through at speed = reckless-driving crime, no charge, barrier down 20 s
- ✅ Speed cameras (Phase 7): 3 fixed sites, flash + $75 fine by SableCam phone message over ≈100 km/h, per-camera + clean-minute cooldowns
- ✅ Destructible roadside props (Phase 7, see §12) feed believable traffic chaos
- ✅ Lasting traffic-accident wrecks (Phase 9): a traffic car at 100% damage becomes a WRECK — engine off, hazard smoulder, and it STAYS: wreck positions persist for the whole game day (`harborfall-wrecks`, day-serial scoped), other traffic diverts around it (a car stuck behind one for 8 s reroutes), and the daily tick clears the roads
- ✅ Roadworks events (Phase 9): one site per game day, rotating across Harbour Ave, Dock Road and the Northgate causeway approach — cones, barriers, a digger and spoil heap, two hi-vis workers, and a real traffic slow zone (cars crawl at ~12 km/h through it); the day's site is announced on Harbour Wire

### 5. NPC & Pedestrian AI
- ✅ Destinations (shops/benches/bus stops), sitting, phone-peds with night glow, dog walker, vendor, rain shelter behaviour, club drift
- ✅ Witness/report pipeline (crimes are seen, reported, then wanted rises)
- ✅ SableSocial (Phase 7): ambient NPC posts react to time, weather and district — and to the player's public crimes
- ✅ Conversations (Phase 9): the four named townsfolk (below) hold real branching dialogue TREES — a greeting (which changes if the district knows you), two choices, written responses, and effects with teeth: a meal bought, a gift that makes nearby peds remember kindness, a reputation cost for being rude. Ambient peds keep their contextual barks on top
- ✅ NPC memory & recognition (Phase 8): per-zone reputation (persisted, decays ×0.55 daily) + per-ped memory — crime witnesses remember for 5 game-minutes and recognise you (subtitle barks, zone-named), pickpocket victims avoid you for the rest of the game-day, helped peds greet you; a remembering witness near a cop points you out (extends the reporting marker + last-known). Witnesses who were helped stay quiet unless alone, and hot-zone witnesses report faster
- ✅ Named-NPC daily schedules (Phase 9): four persistent individuals with names, homes and timetables — Tomas Reed (fisherman: pier at dawn, market midday, club in the evening), Priya Nair (market trader: stall by day, Coral viewpoint at dusk), Bea Kowalski (harbourmaster: HQ steps in the morning, docks at noon, restaurant in the evening), Kip Andersen (skater kid: school corner, park afternoons, pier at night). They walk real waypoint routes between stops (sitting where the stop is a bench), show name labels up close, and are talkable (see Conversations)

### 6. Police & Wanted
- ✅ Line-of-sight + last-known-position search, witness delay, roadblocks, spike strips, helicopter (searchlight ≥3★ at night)
- ✅ Surrender, two ways: hold-B on the street at 1★, and the Harbour Patrol HQ front desk (Phase 7) — same $100 fine, no cell
- ✅ Disguise-lite heat decay (swap car/district while unseen), BUSTED/WASTED flows. Phase 29 honesty check, stated plainly: disguise in this game is ONLY that swap — nothing models identity. Police LOS keys on distance + occlusion, witness memory on the witnessed event, ped recognition on event memory + district reputation; none of it ever reads the player's outfit, haircut or character preset. Changing your look therefore does NOT reset recognition (there is no appearance-based recognition to reset) — its one real effect is on the evidence file below
- ✅ Evidence & heat (Phase 29, game/heat.ts): every crime the patrol learns about — the ones that pass through applyCrime, instantly-known kinds at once and quiet crimes when the witness report lands — adds EVIDENCE to a per-slot file (0..100, `harborfall-heat`): ped 20 · shoot 15 · robbery 15 · assault 10 · steal 8 · theft 6 · hit 5 · reckless 4 (applyCrime's 4 s cooldown throttles gains as it throttles stars). The file sets a WANTED FLOOR — floor = min(3, ⌊evidence/25⌋) — applied to the next reported crime: at 25/50/75 evidence the response starts at 1★/2★/3★ whatever the new crime was. It also LENGTHENS THE SEARCH: the unseen seconds to drop a star grow from 11 s to 11 + evidence × 0.08 s. DECAY: clean (30 s without a known crime) AND unseen ⇒ −1 pt per 4 s; a FRESH LOOK (wardrobe change, Thread & Tide purchase-and-wear, Sable Cuts cut or trim) runs decay at 4× for 120 s. BUSTED or WASTED halves the file (time served); the $100 surrender does NOT (a fine is not time served — deliberate). HUD: a teal 👁 chip with the count beside the wanted stars, only while the file is open; the phone Stats app carries the number, your floor, the Fixer's live quote and the full rules in plain text; Wire headlines fire once per climb at 50 and 90 evidence (the stage re-arms if the file thins below 25)
- ✅ The Fixer (Phase 29): MAREK DOSS, a named phone contact (post-Ch.2), burns the whole file — cost = max($250, $150 + $6×evidence + $200×wanted); on payment all evidence clears, wanted drops to 0, in-flight witness reports are buried and the patrol stands down. Refusals are honest and ordered (mid-mission, mid-escort, cooldown, nothing-to-fix, line-of-sight, funds): he will NOT act while a cop has line of sight on you ("Not while they're looking at you."), charges nothing and consumes nothing on a refusal, and works ONE arrangement per in-game day (the day serial persists with the file; NG+ resets both)
- ✅ Star cap (Phase 9): SIX stars — at 5★, serious witnessed crime (shooting, robbery, running a ped down) builds star HEAT; the third offence raises the 6th star and Dispatch escalates. The sixth star unlocks the Harbour Guard response (below) and slower heat decay; stars still drop one at a time when you break line of sight
- ✅ Police boats / K9 units (Phase 9): the Harbour Patrol BOAT engages at 2★+ whenever you're on the water — it holds station astern and keeps your wanted cooldown pinned (you cannot shake heat on the water while it's on you). The K9 unit works at 3★+ on foot: a handler-and-dog pair that runs you down faster than you walk, and a dog on you fills the bust meter 1.5×. The 6th star adds the Harbour Guard SUV — faster and tougher than the patrol cars
- 🟡 Arrest animations — equivalent: a real hands-up ARREST POSE — as the bust meter fills (or you hold B to surrender) the player rig raises both arms in proportion to the meter. Phase 16 grows it into the ARREST ESCORT: when the meter fills ON FOOT, the nearest patrol officer (a dedicated rig) gets out, walks to you, and walks you — hands up, input locked except camera, subtitle barks throughout — to the patrol car (≤12 s cap); reaching it fires the unchanged BUSTED flow (same fine/impound/respawn). No reachable cop/car, in a vehicle, swimming or indoors → the old instant flow. Surrender (hold B) stays instant/fine-only. There is still no handcuff IK (the rig has no IK for cuffing) and the patrol car is not pinned during the walk, so the line stays 🟡

### 7. Combat & Weapons
- ✅ Fists, pistol, SMG (auto), shotgun (5-pellet) — per-weapon ammo, Q selector, recoil, muzzle light, crouch cover
- ✅ Kestrel Carbine (Phase 33): Sable Arms' own mid-tier automatic — between the SMG and the shotgun in role (0.14 s cycle, 38u reach, tight cone, held-trigger auto like the SMG), $950 at the counter + one mansion-drive pickup, its own procedural model (long receiver, curved mag, skeleton stock, teal sight dot), its own WebAudio voice, and a teal muzzle/tracer tint in the palette's second colour. Ammo is per-weapon-kind in this build (there is no class pool and no rifle class), so the carbine carries its own pool — stated, not dressed as shared. Two fittings on the same bench: suppressor ($420) + match grip/long barrel ($390), applied in the same spec builder as every other mod
- ✅ Cover-lite (Phase 33): the Phase 6 cover geometry (crouched against a wall/car) becomes COMBAT cover with a firearm in hand — a 🛡 IN COVER HUD chip, a deeper crouch tuck, and a PEEK rule: aiming out of cover suspends the relief (police sight falls back to the plain-crouch factor, the bust grab builds at full rate) until you stop aiming. Honest scope: this build's police close to ARREST — no NPC fires at the player anywhere, so there is no NPC accuracy roll for cover to modify; its teeth are the sight and bust maths that exist, and no accuracy system was invented to hang it on. Never engages driving, swimming, or indoors (the pocket rooms' furniture is not in the blocker map the probe reads)
- ✅ Suppression-lite (Phase 33): gunfire breaks bystander nerve — peds within 4.5u of where a shot ends roll 50% to flee, peds within 11u of the muzzle roll 18%, sustained fire stacks +10% per shot inside 3 s (cap +30%, the ageing list is the decay), a suppressed weapon halves both chances; flight runs through the ordinary fleeUntil channel. Police pressure is NOT a second system: every shot still flows through the one witness path (addCrime → applyCrime → Phase 29 heat) — suppression adds no crime, wanted or evidence of its own
- ✅ Hit reactions (flinch/fall) and full crime/witness integration
- ✅ Melee weapon (Phase 8): baseball bat — 5th Q-cycle slot, Lantern Park pickup + $150 at Sable Arms/Coral Pawn, no ammo; wider/slower swing with knockback + longer ped-down (longer still at Strength 50+), wooden thock sfx, same assault crime class as fists
- ✅ Thrown weapons (Phase 9): BOTTLES — a 6th Q slot. Crates of empties by Club Solace and the pier restock you (+6, up to 24 carried, persisted as weapon ammo); click/J lobs one on a real gravity arc — a ped hit is an assault (down 3 s), a car hit dents it, bare ground just scares people, and a bottle through a Market Row shopfront smashes the glass (see §1)
- ✅ Weapon mods/attachments (Phase 9): Sable Arms fitting bench — pistol suppressor + SMG suppressor (shots go through the SOFT crime path: no instant report, witnesses only), SMG extended magazine (faster cycle, tighter spread), pistol reflex sight (longer range, tighter cone), shotgun choke (tighter, longer). One-time cash fittings, persisted (`harborfall-wpmods`), shown as fitted at the counter
- ✅ Free-aim aiming mode (Phase 9): hold right-mouse (or gamepad LT) on foot — over-shoulder camera, narrowed FOV, crosshair; the player turns to the camera, movement slows to a strafe, and Combat fires along the actual camera ray instead of the facing snap. Releasing returns to the normal follow camera

### 8. Missions & Story
- ✅ 6 story chapters, Ch.5 choice with payout consequences, 2 side arcs (Lina's Line, Voss's File), repeatables, debriefs, full subtitles
- ✅ THE HARBOUR HEIST (Phase 7): two-stage chain from Old Elias after Ch.4 — Prep (getaway van check + 2 supply crates; finishing unseen banks the stealth bonus) → The Strongroom (hold-position 6 s drill, alarm trips 3★, escape + lose heat); payout $500 + $1,600 scaled by prep quality (+$150 unseen prep, +$400 clean-prep bonus)
- ✅ Mission checkpoints (Phase 7): step checkpoints recorded live; failing offers retry-from-checkpoint on R with a refreshed clock, alongside full restart from the giver
- ✅ Chapter replay (Phase 7): phone Story app replays completed chapters at 40% payout without touching progress
- ✅ Mission stats (Phase 7): attempts + best clear times per mission in the Stats and Story apps
- 🟡 Branching beyond the Ch.5 choice — equivalent: a SECOND full choice now exists — the post-story pack's publish-vs-burn decision (below) rewrites your reputation city-wide or puts the ledger on the front page, and the heist's prep quality still scales its payout. Phase 15 adds a third, structurally different branch: THE MERIDIAN PAIR, a mutually-exclusive side-mission pair (post-Ch.3) — help Voss's informant OR warn Elias's dock crew about the same shipment; the first call you make accepts that branch and locks the other for the playthrough (persisted per save slot), each with its own dialogue, payout and an opposite Docks-reputation consequence with Harbour Wire coverage. A fully branching mission TREE is still not built — the story stays an authored line with choice points, by design
- ✅ Post-story mission pack (Phase 9): "AFTER THE FALL" — three missions that unlock when the story ends, offered by phone from their givers: Lina's cross-town parts run ($400), Voss's ledger job with the publish-vs-burn CHOICE ($900 to publish / $500 to burn, with real consequences either way), and Elias's night boat run through three bay checkpoints ($700). Tracked in the Story app like the side arcs; finishing all three earns Tamim's message from inside

### 9. Economy & Businesses
- ✅ Cash + SableBank, dealership purchases, gun shops, pawn fencing, vehicle mods, food/heals
- ✅ Buyable businesses (Phase 7): Harbour Wraps stall $3,500 (+$120/day), Northgate Car Wash $6,000 (+$220/day), Club Solace 50% share $12,000 (+$450/day) — hold-E buy points in the world, ownership + income shown in SableBank. Phase 19 grows the line: a FOURTH business — Sable Marina Rentals $15,000 (+$430/day since the Phase 21 balance pass; $380 as shipped in Phase 19), whose office sits on the pier road and whose ownership unlocks the rental dock (game/rentals.ts: a $75 day-pass skiff, returned at the dock, written off if wrecked, collected when the day rolls) — and BUSINESS UPGRADES for all four: levels 1→3 (L2 = 40% of buy price, L3 = 75% since Phase 21; 60%/120% as shipped in Phase 19), each level +35% takings, bought in the Bank app with businessDailyNet as the single source for the shown and paid numbers (old saves default to L1 at identical income).
