Phase 23 grows the line: the Club Solace share pays a WEEKEND DOOR BONUS — +25% of the club's gross on Friday/Saturday nights, inside businessDailyNet as a day-serial-keyed term (the game week is now defined: day serial 0 = Monday; the tick settles the day that just ended under its own serial, the Bank displays the current serial — the same function either way). Phase 41 grows the line again: DAILY BUSINESS EVENTS — each morning every owned business rolls ~35% for a one-day event (Rush +50%, Quiet −30%, Supply snag — halved until the player runs a depot→door supply errand, Inspection — 15% flat fail, $120–$300 fine floored at $0, Staff star — a named hand's bonus doubles), all of it flowing through businessDailyNet so the Bank figure (now with a per-business event badge) is exactly what the tick pays; morning messages from the business's own lead hand, a rush day sometimes in the Wire
- ✅ SableBank loans (Phase 19): borrow $500/$1,500/$4,000 from the Bank app — instant cash, 8% interest per game day on the outstanding balance (total owed capped at 2× principal), the morning tick auto-repays 10% of what's owed from cash on hand (never below $0; a shortfall stays owed), manual part/full repayment in the app, borrowing blocked while a loan is outstanding or at wanted 3★+, debt shown in the Bank app with SableBank messages on borrow and on repayment in full. Persisted per slot in the eco key
- ✅ Daily income tick (Phase 7): ONE consistent rule — the game-day rollover at 06:00 pays all business income to SableBank with a phone message; sleeping runs the same tick (guarded by day serial, never double-pays). Phase 41 grows the line: the same guarded tick also rolls the day's BUSINESS EVENTS for the day that has just started (see the businesses line) — one place does day work, exactly once per day, sleep included
- ✅ Second property (Phase 7): Coral Shores Apartment $5,000 — respawn-point choice, sleep point, garage-save point #2
- ✅ Rental properties (Phase 30, game/economy.ts): a landlord ladder beside the homes — CORAL SHORES RENTAL FLAT ($18,000 → $140/day, door on the mid-rise row at (65, 242.6)), NORTHGATE TOWNHOUSE ($32,000 → $260/day, door at (−59, −236.7)) and DOCKS WAREHOUSE UNIT ($45,000 → $390/day, Unit 7 on the Docks strip at (60, 103.2) — a new frontage building + blocker in the strip's own geometry; Sable Lets leases it only to BUSINESS OWNERS, and the gate is stated at the door and in the Bank, not discovered at the till). Rent pays through the SAME day-serial-guarded advanceDay tick as business income; rentDailyNet()/rentFor() are the single source the tick pays and the Bank displays (a unit with a due bill shows −$due and contributes 0 — shown = paid, the Phase 16 contract). TENANT EVENTS, one roll per let unit per settled day (skipped while a bill is due): ~10% REPAIR ($60–$180 — auto-paid from cash when cash covers it; otherwise it waits as DUE in the Bank, payable at the property's door, and that unit's rent PAUSES until settled; cash never goes below $0), ~12% LATE RENT (half lands), ~10% GOOD TENANT (full rent + 2 district reputation), otherwise full rent quietly. All events arrive as phone messages from the property manager, ANA RUIZ of Sable Lets (a Contacts entry — calling her reads the portfolio live). NO management mini-game is claimed: no tenant picking, no rent setting, no evictions — messages and money only. SELL BACK at 80% of purchase (door hold-E or the Bank's two-step confirm; a due bill is settled out of the proceeds) — rent stops the same day. Persisted per slot as `harborfall-rentals` under its own load guard. NG+ keeps the portfolio with the businesses and homes (ngplus.ts never touches it — a due bill survives with its unit, the debt being part of the property)
- ✅ Sleeping (Phase 7): E at the villa terrace or your apartment → fade to morning, full heal, businesses settle
- ✅ Shop hours (Phase 9): enforced at EVERY counter — Sable Arms (guns + mods), Sable Motors and the Coral dealer (browsing included), Coral Pawn, Thread & Tide, Sable Cuts and Port Ink all refuse with a "closed — opens 07:00" prompt outside 07:00–23:00; Lina's garage and the 24-hour Harbour Wraps vendor keep harbour hours, as posted
- ✅ Stock market, lightweight (Phase 8): SableBank Invest — 3 fictional local stocks (Sable Shipping, Coral Resorts, Northgate Motors), hourly random-walk prices, buy/sell in $100 lots, portfolio value + P/L in the Bank app; prices are nudged by the player's own actions (harbour heist → shipping −8%, race wins → motors +6%, business buys → coral +3%), each nudge announced by a SableBank message. Persisted
- 🟡 Business employees — equivalent: a named staff ROSTER (Phase 15) — each owned business can employ up to 2 people from a 4-person pool (Tom Beck, Marta Reyes, Dev Okafor, Sana Iqbal), each with a one-line trait, their own takings bonus (+40%…+75%) and daily wage ($25…$55) shown in the Bank app before hiring; firing supported, one employer per person, wages/takings settle on the same daily tick (Phase 9–14 anonymous hands migrate to Tom Beck at identical numbers). Phase 19 makes the traits LIVE mechanics: Sana Iqbal's bonus counts double during 20:00–06:00 — settled as a documented day/night split inside businessDailyNet (14 day hours at base + 10 night hours at double), with the Bank app showing her current effective rate recomputed from game time — and Dev Okafor's low wage was already the mechanic it describes. There is still no shift rota or management mini-game, and staff are not simulated in the world — a full management sim is a scope call, not an engine limit, so the line stays 🟡 (see the Phase 19 note). Phase 21 gives staff an IN-WORLD presence (game/staffworld.ts + components/StaffWorld.tsx, on the Phase 9 townsfolk pattern — no new character system): every hired staffer appears at their business's frontage during fixed working hours (Sana on the night shift 20:00–06:00, matching her trait; Dev part-time 10:00–18:00; Tom 07:00–19:00; Marta 08:00–20:00), pacing a short beat in a distinct work colour, with a floating name + business + trait label when near and a greeting bark once per day when approached; they despawn cleanly off-shift, on being let go, or if the business leaves the portfolio, and the Bank app marks who is "on shift now". What remains for a full ✅ is unchanged: shifts are fixed per person (no rota simulation) and there is no management play beyond hire/fire — a scope call, so the line stays 🟡 (see the Phase 21 note). Phase 41 checked this line honestly: the daily business events make individual staff MATTER more (a star day doubles a named hand's bonus; the lead hand speaks for the business each morning), but events are weather for the portfolio, not rota or management play — the 🟡 reason is unchanged (see the Phase 41 note)

### 10. Activities
- ✅ Taxi jobs (Phase 7): fares hail the cab (waving ped + marker), ride along, fare by distance + tip for speed/cleanliness, 1–5★ passenger rating, earnings tracked
- ✅ Delivery jobs (Phase 7): Lina's 3-leg chain formalised in the Jobs app — per-leg pay + $150 chain bonus
- ✅ Street races (Phase 7): Docks Sprint, Bay Run (over the bridge), Northgate Loop — 3 AI rivals on the route, checkpoint sequence, live position, $600/$250/$100 prizes, optional $100 bet (win = $200 back), best times persisted. Phase 18 grows the line twice: DRIFT SCORING (handbrake slides build a chained score — angle × speed × time, ×2–×5 chain multipliers, banked on a clean release for cash + Driving ticks, lost whole on a collision; the slip angle is an honest estimate from steering lock + speed, documented in game/drift.ts, since the arcade model has no velocity vector) and THE SABLE CUP — the 3 street races + Bay Circuit as a 4-round championship armed from Phone → Jobs (points 10/6/3/1, best finish counts, ≥20/40 crowns a champion: $2,500 + a Wire headline + a one-use gold Vantara S voucher at −20%; Street Challenge pot races are a different format and never count, by design). Phase 34 adds THE SABLE SLIDE SERIES — a 3-round DRIFT championship on the Cup's structure (per-slot progress, sequential unlock, arm in Phone → Jobs, conversion at the course ring): gated courses (Docks Yard 500 pts/120 s, Bay Infield 800/150 s, Northgate Interchange 1,000/120 s) where the gates must be threaded in order inside the clock AND the target banked on the way — the Series counts only drift.ts BANKS (off DRIFT.bankSeq), so a collision still loses the open chain before it can score; champion takes $3,000, a headline, the Stats → Records title and SLIDE GOLD, a champion-only swatch in Lina's existing respray palette (career honour, survives a series reset). And a RECORDS board in Phone → Stats gathering the best drift chain, Slide round bests, all five race bests (jobStats.bestTimes, written by every finish block), derby best/wins and the best catch in one place; drift scores are deliberately NOT posted to the Python leaderboard — its race_best is a lower-wins time and a points total is not a time (race bests already flow via postRaceResult)
- ✅ Collectible tokens, photo mode, swimming, island trips
- ✅ Random world events — now 10 (Phase 7 adds the rushing ambulance to the clinic: lights flashing, Dispatch bark, live map marker; Phase 17 adds STREET CHALLENGE — a parked exotic driver offers a $200 point-to-point pot race on the real race state — and SPILLED CARGO — 60 s of collectable crates at a road spot while traffic swerves around the spill; both run on the shared G.eventNow courtesy with the B2 events; Phase 24 adds the FISHING DERBY — a 3-minute scored contest at the Pier on the same courtesy: enter free at the pier ring, every catch in the window scores its sale value (shore or boat), beat the house score for $300 + a Harbour Wire headline, best score persisted in Stats; Phase 47 adds the two DAY events on the advanceDay roll — the HARBOUR REGATTA, a buoy time trial in the bay with its best in Records and a once-a-day purse, and the CORAL STREET MARKET, three stalls with a 25%-off bench voucher and a +20% fence — day-scoped beside the business events, one per day at most, never both)
- ✅ Fishing (Phase 8): pier + Sable Island dock spots — hold/release cast with a sweet zone, bite window, hold-to-reel marker mini (line snaps if you over-reel); 5 fish (Harbour Sprat, Sable Mackerel, day Coral Trout, night Night Eel, rare Golden Koi) with sizes by spot/time of day; sell the creel at Harbour Wraps; best catch + totals in Stats, persisted. Phase 24 takes the rod to the water: FISH FROM BOATS — seated in any still boat (owned, borrowed or a marina rental) you can cast over the side; the boat is a mobile spot (stand point = the hull, cast point = 7u ahead of the bow) fishing the OFFSHORE table (island-rich weighting plus a sixth fish, the offshore-only Sable Tuna, day or night); starting the engine, stepping out or a bust/wasted ends the session cleanly, and the creel is the same bucket, sold at the Wraps as before
- ✅ Gym (Phase 8): Sable Fitness OUTDOOR corner at Lantern Park (chosen over a pocket interior — cheaper, in the world) — bench press (Strength) + treadmill (Stamina) rhythm sessions: tap E in the moving zone ×8, $10, once per game-day per station, faster skill gains than ambient use; 50+ Strength makes fists/bat drop peds longer
- ✅ Tow jobs (Phase 8): Jobs app tow shift — Sable Wrecker at Lina's; a stranded car is marked, reverse the hook in and hold still to hitch, tow on a swinging tether (wrecker accel cut while hooked), deliver into Lina's garage zone for $150; stats tracked
- ✅ Valet jobs (Phase 22): the odd-jobs board in Phone → Jobs — a customer's car waits at one of 3 hotel/club frontages (Club Solace, Hotel Northgate, Coral Shores Resort); take the keys and park it in the stand's bay before the clock runs out: $90 base − $6 per point of damage the car takes on the run (floor $20), +$30 no-damage bonus; the car is a dynamic unowned entry on the rental discipline (collected on finish/fail/timeout — on step-out if you're still in it; a wreck fails the job and stays in the world); 5 s out-of-car grace, stats tracked
- ✅ Courier sprint (Phase 22): 3 drop points in sequence, on foot or bicycle only — each leg has its own distance-scaled countdown, which PAUSES while you sit in a car (feet-and-pedals work; the marker label says so); $45 per drop on arrival + $60 full-chain bonus; a timed-out leg closes the chain with drops already paid kept; starting a new chain replaces the old one cleanly (Phase 23 audit: the replacement is now real — the start gate used to refuse it while a chain ran); stats tracked
- ✅ Paramedic job (Phase 32): the dispatched-ambulance concept, player-side — start a shift at the hospital door (wanted must be 0, jobBusy-gated); the job ambulance (new kind: Sable Medic Van) spawns on the hospital lot on the rental discipline (never ownable/storable, collected at shift end, wreck ends the shift and stays); patients wait at the dispatch sim's own roadside medical spots (a lying figure + the job marker), nearest-first so the three legs escalate (Ferry Quarter → Docks strip → Market Row); reach them on a generous distance-scaled clock, treat on foot (hold-E 3 s channel), drive them back to the hospital bay; pay per patient $110 + up to $60 time bonus (return leg's remaining fraction) + $40 no-damage bonus, +$100 for a full board, all through the NG+ payout multiplier; a patient lost on the clock is lost honestly (no pay, the board moves on); jobStats: patients delivered, shifts, service earnings
- ✅ Firefighter job (Phase 32): start at the NEW Sable Fire Station on the Docks strip (its own blocker + frontage, door on the z=103.2 line); the job fire tender (new kind: Sable Fire Tender) spawns on the civic lot, same rental discipline; fires ignite at the dispatch sim's fire frontages (Ferry Quarter → south-west grid) with a new flame/smoke presentation (flickering cones + rising smoke + a fire light, size = strength); park the tender within 9u, hose on foot (hold-E): strength drains over ~6 s of water with a droplet arc + steam at the base; the tank holds 100 and a fire drinks 55, so a refill (hold-E 2.5 s at the marked nearest hydrant — real hydrant props from the street scatter — or the station door, tender parked close) is genuinely required between the shift's two fires; a fire left 240 s burns out unpaid; pay $160 + up to $70 speed bonus per fire, +$120 for both out; sirens on both service jobs (beacons + the shared siren voice while you drive on a call) are wanted-neutral and taking the unit is never a steal or a break-in; traffic courtesy is the Phase 22 horn path (hornPull kerb-ease ahead of the unit — visual only; there is no police-yield hook and none is claimed); bust/wasted/NG+/wreck/walk-away all stand the shift down through the established paths; dispatcher barks + phone summaries from "Dispatch — Ren" (a phone contact now, too)
- ✅ Taxi passenger personalities (Phase 23): every fare rolls a type at hail time — Calm (40%, the long-standing baseline maths), Hurried (the speed bonus pays +40% of fare instead of +25% when you land with >25% of the par still in hand), Nervous (ANY damage to the cab on the ride zeroes the tip; they complain the moment it happens and again at the kerb), Chatty (standard maths + 2–3 original subtitle barks across the ride). The type is named in the marker label and the hail toast, the boarding bark states the deal, ALL tip maths lives in settleFare (game/jobs.ts) — the one place a tip is computed — and the completion toast + Sable Cabs message show the fare + tip breakdown; jobStats gains a lifetime tips total

### 11. Smartphone / Interface
- ✅ Core apps: Contacts, Messages, SableBank, Music, Maps, Garage, Stash, Stats, Camera, Settings
- ✅ Jobs app (Phase 7): live taxi/delivery status, start-delivery button, race bet toggle, race best times, job earnings
- ✅ Story app (Phase 7): chapter list with lock/complete state, replay buttons, side-arc + heist status
- ✅ News app (Phase 7): Harbour Wire — headlines generated from the player's actual actions (mission completions, chases, purchases, the heist, weather turns), rolling persisted feed
- ✅ Social app (Phase 7): SableSocial — ambient posts + reactions to public crimes (see §5)
- ✅ GPS route (Phase 7): the big map draws a dashed route polyline (Manhattan routing on the road grid; bridge/causeway spines cross-district) to the active mission or job target
- ✅ Game flow: staged loading, splash, spawn sweep, area cards, respawn mini-screens, door fades, no-select solid HUD, wind line in the weather chip tooltip + phone home
- ✅ Gamepad + touch input (Phase 8): standard-mapping gamepad (analog move/steer, right-stick camera, triggers, full button map in Help, connect toast, last-input-wins with keyboard/touch); rebuilt floating analog touch joystick (appears at the thumb, deadzone + expo curve, sprint latch, gyro yields while deflected)
- ✅ Smoothness (Phase 8): dynamic resolution ladder (DPR 1.1–2 by tier cap), continuous adaptive tier drop with manual-override respect, HUD snapshot at 5 Hz, "Show FPS" option in phone Settings + pause Graphics. (Phase 45: the Low tier is a full weak-phone preset — shadows off, DPR cap 1.0, tier ceilings on the traffic/ped density levers, wildlife at half rate — and the start-screen poster art is a lazy chunk, cutting initial load to ≈629 kB gzip.)
- ✅ In-phone internet/browser (Phase 9): SABLEWEB — a real in-phone browser with five pages rendered from LIVE game state, not canned text: a home hub, Sable Motors (the actual dealer stock and prices), a weather page (current conditions, wind and the forecast), the Harbour Wire front page (your live news feed), and Classifieds (the real dealer + business listings with prices). It is deliberately a closed local web — Port Sable's intranet, not a fake copy of the open internet
- ✅ Camera gallery persistence (Phase 9): photo-mode captures now ALSO save a downscaled copy to the phone's GALLERY app (newest 10, persisted in localStorage, tap to view full-size, deletable) — the full-res PNG download still happens exactly as before
- ✅ Sable Tunes music player (Phase 35): the phone's Music app becomes a real player (game/tunes.ts) — four original WebAudio compositions as selectable tracks (the opening theme, the Club Solace loop, and two new loops composed this phase), play/pause + prev/next, honest elapsed + loop-position readouts, per-slot persisted selection, club priority (the room suspends the phone's track and resumes it after), and the global N mute as the one audio switch. (Phase 46: the AMBIENT SCORE (game/score.ts) joins the same discipline as its lowest-priority citizen — layered procedural loops keyed to game state: a pad bed in the theme's chord family, a pluck layer that follows nightfall, a pulse layer at 3★+ that quickens at 5★+, with the pad shifting to the relative-minor progression while wanted; a playing Tunes track or a room hold silences it, Settings → Music → Ambient score turns it off, and the N mute covers it like every music source.)
- ✅ Gallery grows (Phase 35): the keep limit rises to the last 12 shots, every new capture is labelled with its district (districtAt, streamed districts included), and the app gains a full-screen viewer (tap a shot; district + date + delete) — the stored copy stays a 320 px JPEG thumbnail (~15–35 KB) by design, the full-res PNG download is unchanged

### 12. World Simulation
- ✅ Day cycle, 5-state weather, shop hours, rush hours, streaming activation + far-ped tick skip. Phase 22 grows the rush hours into a CITY RHYTHM (game/rhythm.ts): one rhythmFactor(hour) — ×1.3 at 07:00–09:00 and 17:00–19:00, ×0.7 at 00:00–05:00, ×1 otherwise — multiplies BOTH density paths (traffic share and ped crowd fraction) on top of the player's Phase 16 levers, capped at the High-density caps, with the standing exemptions untouched (service/dispatch/bus/taxi/cops/staff/named NPCs are never rhythm-culled); the traffic loop's old private rush window and the peds' flat +0.25 rush bump are retired into it, the Cityliner dwell shortens at rush (4.5 s → 3.2 s), and a "Rush hour" chip shows on the HUD clock line during peaks
- ✅ Horn reactions (Phase 22): the player's horn (B / touch HORN / gamepad RB, via game/horn.ts) is heard by the street — peds within ~8u startle with a step-aside hop on the existing knock-velocity channel plus the arms-up flinch pose, and a traffic car directly ahead within ~10u may pull toward the kerb for ~2 s through the Phase 17 swerve-offset path (visual only; AI lane/route/speed untouched). A 1.5 s reaction cooldown caps the chaos; service units, the bus and dispatched cars don't react, cops aren't traffic, and a honk never raises wanted. Documented as what it is: a cosmetic life detail
- ✅ Wind gusts (Phase 7): one slow gust value drives palm-frond sway, boat bob/pitch/roll, and a wind label (Calm/Breezy/Windy/Gusting) in the HUD chip + phone
- ✅ Fish (Phase 7): visible fish circling and jumping at the pier, island shallows and Coral shore — Phase 42: the dolphin pod leans on the catch roll's existing rarity roll (no fake system bolted on): pod within 40u of the cast point doubles the rare-koi chance (3% → 6%) and weights the pool rich like island water; Elias barks "Dolphins — good sign." when you cast into water the pod is working
- ✅ Destructible props (Phase 7): crates/barrels/fences at the docks, yards, fuel points and the Lantern Park construction line — hit at speed in a car and they break into flying debris; repaired on the next game day
- ✅ The daily tick (Phase 7): day rollover pays businesses and resets destructibles
- ✅ Seasons (Phase 13) — **seasons fully simulated; snow absent by climate, not by omission.** A season lasts 7 game-days (derived from the persisted economy day serial) and turns the whole climate: the sun's arc and the day's length (summer rides high and long, winter low and short — the warped elevation feeds the night factor, ped density and every light in the city), sky/fog tint multipliers, water tint, the instanced street-tree foliage (spring fresh green + cream blossom, summer deep green, autumn amber, winter grey-green — palms stay green all year, they are palms), and the weather odds themselves (summer: more Clear + a ×1.8 Storm bonus on 13:00–18:00 rolls; winter: Fog/Cloudy-leaning, cooler light). Season turns are announced on Harbour Wire + SableSocial; the player can pin Auto/Spring/Summer/Autumn/Winter in Pause → Season or Phone → Settings (persisted in the settings save). Winter in Port Sable is cool, grey and dry — a subtropical harbour city does not get snow, and a white palette swap would be a fake, so there is none
- 🟡 Persistent world state — equivalent: DAY-SCOPED persistence (Phase 9) — traffic wrecks, smashed shopfront glass and broken destructible props now persist for the whole game day (saved with the day serial, restored on reload, repaired/cleared on the daily tick); the player, economy, jobs, stats, story, look and gallery persist across sessions as before. Phase 13 adds LAST-VEHICLE persistence: the car you last used (kind, colour, position, heading, damage, fuel, window, tune/brake levels, flat tyre) is snapshotted on entry, refreshed at the wheel and finalised on exit (`harborfall-lastcar`), restored where you left it next session, and marked with an amber YOUR CAR ring on the big map. Phase 16 grows it into the PARKED FLEET: up to 3 OWNED cars persist the same way (`harborfall-fleet`, the 3 most recently used — a pre-Phase-16 owned last-car migrates in as entry #1), each with its own amber ring on the big map, numbered when there's more than one; non-owned cars keep the single last-car behaviour. The documented clearing rules (per car): wrecked (100% damage), impounded when you're busted, or put away into a garage (it lives in the garage system then). What still resets per session is ambient traffic/ped placement — full cross-session world simulation is a scope call, not an engine limit

*Phase 11 note (reflections — no tally change): the "ray tracing" request landed as **ray-traced-style reflections** inside the existing graphics ✅ lines (quality tiers / PMREM environment reflections / post-processing, §§1–13): screen-space reflections on glossy surfaces + a planar-reflection bay on High, a per-tier probe-strength scale on glass towers and car paint, and a player override + mode readout (Off / Standard / Ray-traced-style) in Pause → Graphics and Phone → Settings. Hardware ray tracing does not exist in WebGL — the honest account is in `docs/VISUAL_AUDIT.md` (Phase 11). No §15 line changes state: reflections were already counted, they are now dynamic instead of probe-only on High.*

*Phase 13 note (this round — tally 82/9/2 → 84/9/0, the ledger closes): the last two ⬜ lines fall. **Trains** lands as the Sable Line light-rail tram (§15.3) — the city-scale form of the topic, chosen over a heavy-rail network because Port Sable's plan has no rail corridor and the elevated loop reuses the road geography the player already knows. **Seasons / snow** lands as a fully simulated seasonal system (§15.12) with snow deliberately absent: the topic's "seasons" half is implemented end-to-end (sun, light, foliage, weather odds, calendar, settings pin), and the "snow" half is answered by climate — Port Sable is subtropical; the line is ✅ with the reasoning on the line itself rather than a 🟡, because nothing about the season system is a lightweight equivalent. The two 🟡 lines that grew: enterable buildings (6 → 8 pocket interiors) and persistent world state (last-vehicle persistence with documented clearing rules). Phase 12 (optimisation — fixed-timestep sim, AI staggering, instancing, code-splitting) changed no line states; Phase 13 was built on its structure and does not regress it (guideway/tram geometry is instanced, the tram path is pre-sampled with zero per-frame allocation, and the new code rides the existing static world-logic chunk like Phases 7–9).*

*Phase 14 note (this round — tally 84/9/0 → 86/7/0): two 🟡 lines close outright. **Ragdoll** is a genuine verlet solver now (C++ core + mirror + smoke tests), scoped honestly to the near field — the line is ✅ with the scope on the line. **Input remapping** lands as a real Settings surface with conflict detection, stored per save slot. Three 🟡 lines grow without closing: enterable buildings (8 → 10 pocket interiors — the gym indoors and a second purchasable home), destructible buildings (the shared breakage set grows to 43 props + 15 glass panes, and the bat/bottle join the car as breaking tools), and persistent world state (three save slots now namespace every harborfall-* key per profile). The two new activities (Bay Circuit, Sparrow Run) reuse the Phase 7 race state machine — no parallel systems — and the street-race player-progress measurement was corrected to the active vehicle in passing (it previously read the on-foot position, which does not track the car). The same release carries the HIGH-TIER STABILITY PASS from a player's crash report (black screen + blue normal-buffer frame + 2.7 GB tab on a Retina Mac): the composer now remounts cleanly on tier/reflection changes instead of hot-swapping SSR inside a live composer, an error boundary falls back to plain rendering for the session on any composer error, WebGL context loss resets graphics to a safe state with a plain-language toast, DPR caps drop (High 2 → 1.5, Medium 1.5 → 1.35), SSR marches cheaper (0.5 → 0.35 scale, 20/5 → 12/3 steps) and never runs alongside SSAO, the planar-water target is hard-capped at 1024×512, High CC0 textures cap at 768 px, and reflections default OFF with a one-time migration (reflSafeV) so an existing crashing configuration never greets its owner again. Phase 12's structure is preserved throughout: ragdoll steps inside the fixed sim step, no new eager chunks (initial-load gzip stays in the ~770 kB class), instancing patterns untouched.*

*Phase 15 note (this round — §15 tally unchanged at 86/7/0; two §-level lines outside §15 close): the round opens with a PLAYABILITY pass driven by a "not properly playable" verdict on the live Phase 14 build. Fixed defects: (1) persisted-key loads in store.ts/missionState.ts shared one try{} each — one corrupt JSON silently skipped every later load including settings + the reflSafeV migration; each key now loads under its own guard. (2) Key remapping accepted reserved movement keys — binding Jump to W killed forward movement (Player/Vehicle read WASD raw, before bind translation); WASD/arrows/Tab/Escape are now refused at capture and sanitized out of existing saves. (3) The showroom counter's keyup checked the physical E key while its keydown used the interact binding — with interact remapped, tap-to-browse never fired; both halves read the binding now. (4) detectTier handed High to every desktop that didn't report deviceMemory (Safari/Firefox report none and got the old `?? 8` default); High now requires reported memory ≥ 8 AND ≥ 8 cores AND a fine pointer, everything else starts Medium (saved tiers untouched), and a manual raise to High is sample-checked for 2 s (sustained <30 FPS steps back to Medium with the standard toast). (5) CC0 photo textures preloaded on High before the first playable frame; the defer now covers every tier and releases on idle after spawn. (6) The DPR ladder gains a 1.0 floor. (7) The ragdoll step/pose (peds + player WASTED) runs under guards — a solver fault degrades that one body to the static collapse instead of throwing out of the frame loop. (8) A global error net toasts "Something glitched — the game kept running" (throttled) instead of a silent frozen canvas. Verified clean and left alone: the save-slot prefix layer (migration idempotent, no double-prefix, no pre-layer reads — main.tsx import order holds), hold-E paths under default binds, and the fixed-timestep accumulators (debt already clamped/dropped). Part B grows four §15 🟡 lines without closing them (enterable buildings 10 → 11 interiors with the Thread & Tide walk-in; countryside grows the Sable Downs farm network; branching grows the mutually-exclusive Meridian Pair; business employees grow from one anonymous hand to a named 4-person roster, up to 2 per business) and closes two §-level lines: Building interiors (its last 🟡 sub-item, the shop) and Emergency-services dispatch (lite dispatch on the existing traffic units). Phase 12/14 structure preserved: dispatch rides the traffic loop, initial-load gzip stays in the ~790 kB class.*

*Phase 16 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 86/7/0; five 🟡 lines grow): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 15 in the Phase 15 Part A style. Three defects found and fixed: (1) DISPATCH STALL — wreck a dispatched ambulance/fire unit to 100% and its incident never cleared: GameSystems' traffic loop `continue`s on the wreck check before the dispatch branch, so stepDispatchDrive never ran again and no new incident ever generated. tickDispatch now carries a watchdog (unit wrecked or order lost → stand down, bark, reschedule). (2) MERIDIAN PAIR SOFT-LOCK — pairChoice was set at mission START and completion was recorded nowhere; lastFailed is memory-only, so a failed/abandoned attempt (or a reload) locked BOTH branches for the playthrough. M.pairDone (persisted per slot) now separates the lock from completion; the unfinished branch is re-offered by its own giver, the other stays locked, and the call-time headline/cold-call/consequence fires stay once-only (headline lives in the first-call branch; consequences in the completion branch). (3) FARM SOUTH SPUR — its comment cited a "z=−40 city road" that does not exist (ROAD_COORDS has no −40) and a Ferry Quarter house clipped its east edge; verified by running the world generation headlessly — the spur is re-sited to x=−79.5 with zero building/blocker overlap, and the comment now says what is true (it ends at the block edge; a house at (−80,−30) bars a dirt continuation). Verified clean in the same audit: staffing (Bank UI and the daily tick both read businessDailyNet — the number shown is the number paid; one-employer enforced in the UI write path; anonymous hands migrate to Tom Beck at identical numbers), Thread & Tide (door hours at the street door, counter inside the pocket, exit returns to the door + 1.2), farm spawns (32 spawns, ids sequential; the farm pickup at id 31 unblocked on dirt; all 8 incident spots unblocked), dispatch geometry (leg turns, lane re-entry, 150 s stand-down, generation grid-bounded), and the beacon sync (a visibility toggle inside the existing traffic forEach — no per-frame allocation). PART A also lands the PLAYER PERFORMANCE LEVERS: trafficDensity / pedDensity / viewDistance in G.settings (persisted per slot via harborfall-settings), surfaced in Pause → Settings and Phone → Settings, read live — traffic via per-car density ranks assigned in initWorld over the ambient pool (share 0.5 at Low; service units, bus and taxis unranked/exempt; dispatched units, wrecks and anything within 25u never culled) plus the rush-reserve rule at High; peds via a multiplier on the time-of-day crowd fraction (×0.5 / ×1 / ×1.35, downed peds and the reporting witness force-active); view distance via the streaming radii (traffic 165/140 and the ped hold 170, ×0.65/×1/×1.45) and the Weather fog distances (×0.78/×1/×1.3). Defaults Normal/Normal/Normal reproduce the pre-Phase-16 numbers exactly, and the Phase 15 playability guards (per-key load guards, reserved keybinds, error net, tier rules) are untouched. PART B: the ARREST ESCORT (game/escort.ts) — a filled on-foot bust meter starts a walked scene to the patrol car instead of the instant overlay, built from the ped-walk steering, the existing hands-up pose and the bark subtitle system, capped at 12 s, completing into the untouched BUSTED flow; FARM LIFE — farmhouse interior #12 with the farmhand's bunk (free sleep, no setHomePref: the bunk is a workplace perk, not a property — documented on the line and in the room) and FARM WORK on the delivery-chain pattern with its own J flags (crates stack 2/4/6 in the pickup bed across the shift); the PARKED FLEET (harborfall-fleet, ≤3 owned cars, per-entry clearing identical to last-car, migration of an owned last-car to entry #1, numbered big-map rings); BARBER VARIETY (4 new cuts + 3 beard styles as rig geometry — beard cards + the painted stubble variant — on the existing LOOK layer). Phase 12/14/15 structure preserved: the escort steps inside the fixed sim, the officer is one extra rig, initial-load gzip stays in the ~795 kB class (799.1 kB measured).*

*Phase 17 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 86/7/0; three ✅ lines grow): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 16 in the established style. Two defects found and fixed: (1) ESCORT STATE-CHANGE LEAK — Phase 16 guarded the escort's START (on foot, outdoors, not swimming) but never re-checked state mid-scene; the E-interact chain stays live during the walk, so the player could enter a car or an interior door mid-escort: Player's escort branch requires !driving, so the rig stopped following the officer, yet the scene kept stepping and its 12 s cap fired finishEscort() — a BUSTED landing on a player who had driven away or stepped indoors. stepEscort now cancels the scene on ANY of driving/swimming/interior/status (cancel releases the "Under arrest" hold; the bust pin is re-derived every step from ESCORT.active, so ordinary bust rules resume cleanly — a slow player beside a cop can still be re-grabbed fairly, and no path fires BUSTED twice: finishEscort clears ESCORT.active before calling onBusted, and GameSystems' instant path is else-if behind the escort pin). The officer's spawn point is also walked toward the player until clear of blockers, so they never step out inside a wall. (2) FLEET DUPLICATION — applyCarSave's dynamic path only reused storedAway cars before pushing a fresh one; the garage-kept car (loaded earlier in store.ts as a live push) could therefore be duplicated by a fleet entry for the same car. A dynamic restore now first reuses a live car of the same kind+colour within 2u of the snapshot. Verified clean in the same audit: density culling (cops are a separate system; dispatched units carry no density rank and the cull branch also skips them explicitly; mission vehicles are player cars, never traffic; service/bus/taxi units are unranked and exempt; culled cars rejoin via the normal 165/140 streaming hysteresis), the ped factor (named townsfolk are Phase 9's own rigs, not G.peds; downed peds and the reporting witness are force-active over the factor), fleet ordering (garage → stored list → last-car → fleet all run through the one applyCarSave with the sameCarSave double-apply guard; bust impound clears only the driven car's entry), farm work (its J flags are checked by the shared jobBusy() alongside delivery/tow; the crate stack hides the step the shift ends), and the barber LOOK layer (clearLookOverride fires on character AND variant change in App; the character-select showcase renders the same rig + LOOK, so the preview shows the owned cut/beard). PART B: THE HARBOUR WIRE (missions.ts SIDE_MISSIONS, side key "wire", M.side.wire persisted under the per-key load guards) — wire1 "Dead Drops and Coffee" is a new mission KIND, "photo": a reach step at Café Coral, then three evidence spots that arm in the ring and complete on a real photo-mode capture (App stamps G.lastPhotoAt from the same capturePhoto path the Gallery uses — no fake camera); wire2 "The Courier's Loop" is kind "tail": a dedicated courier van (not a traffic unit) drives the loop axis-aligned on the road grid at ~7.2 u/s with 3.2 s stops, the player holding a 12–45 m band (the G.hold meter shows live distance); >2.5 s inside 10 m spooks him, >6 s beyond 55 m loses him — both fail through failMission with checkpoint retry, and a missed stop simply comes back around the loop; wire3 "Print Run" is a timed delivery at forceWanted 2 to the Market Row newsroom point. Each part lands Harbour Wire headlines (pushNews) and an Ines phone message on completion; the arc-complete case is checked before completeMission's generic ≥3 line (which names only Lina/Voss). THE RIDEABLE BUS (game/bus.ts + components/BusRide.tsx): BUS_ROUTE gains the East Yards stop and South Market becomes a stop — four stops with kerbside street points (two reuse the world's existing bus-stop POIs); boarding claims the prompt politely (the component mounts before Tram and only writes a free/owned line), the fare is $2, riding writes playerPos/followPos/playerHeading exactly like the tram, Player renders the seated pose at G.busSeatY, and GameSystems' two wanted gates (heat bleed, bust) now also require G.busRide < 0 — the tram's treatment mirrored, not a second system. The big map draws amber stop squares and a live bus square (traffic dots stay small/white). WORLD EVENTS (components/Phase17.tsx): a private scheduler on the shared G.eventNow courtesy (never starts during a mission/race/status/another event; sets G.eventNow while live so the B2 events and the ambulance rush stand off, and vice versa) — STREET CHALLENGE parks an original hyper exotic + driver at one of three wide-road spots; accepting while driving takes the $200 stake and seeds J.race directly (one rival, one destination ≥120u away, RaceDef.challenge pays winner-takes-the-pot $400 in Phase7's finish block; the rival renders in the race system's supercar class — the parked show car is the hyper); SPILLED CARGO scatters five crates at a road spot for 60 s, collected on foot for $15–40 or stash loot (pawn-fenceable), while GameSystems' traffic loop gives nearby cars a decaying visual swerve offset around the point (AI lane state untouched). Both events are fixed owned meshes — the density levers cannot cull them. Phase 12/14/15/16 structure preserved: everything new steps inside the existing frame loops, no new per-frame allocations of note, initial-load gzip stays in the ~800 kB class (806.5 kB measured).*

*Phase 18 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 86/7/0; two ✅ lines grow): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 17 in the established style. Four defects found and fixed: (1) PHOTO FARMING — wire1's evidence arm was set on entering a spot's ring and survived leaving it, while the banking check only compared capture timestamps: arm a spot, walk anywhere, photograph anything, walk back in, and the out-of-ring capture banked as evidence. The arm now disarms the moment the player leaves the ring; the counting capture must be taken inside it, in one visit. (2) EVENT COURTESY, BOTH DIRECTIONS BROKEN — GameSystems' B2 events never checked G.eventNow/J.race/G.incident before starting (they could open over a Street Challenge, a live race or a dispatch call), and on expiry B2 cleared G.eventNow UNCONDITIONALLY, silently killing a live Phase 17 event's flag (cargo swerve ended early, the slot freed under a live event). B2 now starts only when the shared contract is clear and clears only its own "performer" flag; Phase17's scheduler also stands off for dispatch incidents now. (3) TAIL RETRY ACCOUNTING — startPlayAt rebuilt M.collected as {0..step-1}, but tail stops bank in loop order and can be held out of sequence after a missed pass, so a retry could credit stops never confirmed; failMission now snapshots the real collected set into M.lastFailed (threaded through the App retry handler, which clears lastFailed before calling) and the courier rejoins its loop AT the current stop instead of restarting at stop 0. (4) WIRE3 HEAT — forceWanted raised a story 2★ at Print Run's start and nothing ever cleared it: a successful delivery left a phantom chase with no narrative cause, and a bust could still land after the exposé was safe. The wire-arc completion branch now clears the heat the mission itself raised (documented on the line: heat from the player's own crimes on the run is wiped with it — the run is the run). Verified clean in the same audit: the bus (boarding only while the unit dwells at a stop — atStop requires stopT > 0; fare refused under $2 and charged once per boarding; alighting reuses the tram's street points, two of them the world's existing HAIL_POINTS kerbs; both wanted gates mirror the tram's exactly; the bus carries busRoute and is excluded from the density pool filter), the Street Challenge (stake taken once — the claim requires !J.race and ending the event clears the prompt; the pot pays once in the finish block, which nulls J.race; abandoning pays nothing and the "abandoned" message is correct because challenge races carry bet 0), Spilled Cargo (crates hide when the event ends; the swerve offset decays in the traffic loop regardless of event state), the tail loop itself (all four stops sit on ROAD_COORDS lines; the van's axis-aligned x-then-z driving reaches each in turn; failMission nulls the courier and clears the "Tail:" hold), and the Phase 17 Part A fixes (escort mid-scene cancel on driving/swimming/interior/status; fleet restore dedupe within 2u). PART B: DRIFT SCORING (game/drift.ts) — the arcade physics model drives the car along its heading and has no velocity vector, so there is no true slip angle to measure; the scored angle is an ESTIMATE from steering lock + speed while the handbrake is held (the same class of honest fake as the Phase 10 body roll), and drift.ts says so on its face. Segments chain within 2.5 s up to ×5; banking needs a full clean second after release (cash = points/12, a Driving tick at 200+, best/total in jobStats with Stats + Jobs rows); any collision damage while a chain is open loses it (Vehicle feeds the step's damage delta); the meter rides the existing ~8 Hz mini-UI poll — no new per-frame React state; rear-drive kinds score at 1.25–1.3× via DRIFT_FACTOR, utility kinds less. THE SABLE CUP (game/cup.ts) — arm-then-race: the Jobs card arms a round, the normal start prompt converts the arm into RaceState.cup (any other start discards it), finishes record positions against the existing fields (street: the computed place; Bay Circuit: 1st/2nd vs the single ghost — the whole field there is), points 10/6/3/1, best finish counts while the series is open; at 4/4 rounds the series closes — ≥20/40 crowns the champion ($2,500, a Harbour Wire headline, and the gold voucher: dealerPrice() in cup.ts prices the Vantara S at −20% through BOTH dealer counters, consumeVoucher() spends it once at the till; an unspent voucher survives a series reset). Street Challenge races can never count: Phase17 seeds them directly into J.race, bypassing the start prompts where arms convert — documented in jobs.ts and cup.ts. TWO NEW ORIGINALS — the SABLE KESTREL RS (dealer-only, $18,000: the coupe shell track-trimmed — lower/wider, splitter, fixed rear wing, fat rear rubber; handling 86, the range's sharpest; drift factor 1.3) and the BRONTO HAULER ($9,500 at the dealer + a farm spawn by the barn — NOT the Farm Work vehicle, which stays bound to spawn id 31; capped at 14 u/s for torque; its dirt grip bonus applies on Vehicle's surface path into step_vehicle_v3, 0.72 → ~0.9; one ambient traffic unit on the route nearest the farm corner wears the kind — traffic is grid-bound and has no rural stream to join). Both flow through CAR_MODELS/CarBody SHELL+CABIN_POS/BODY_DIMS, the dealer list, garage storage, fleet and last-car like every kind before them. TIER-3 TUNING — brakes were the ladder's unfinished half (one tier since Phase 6): BRAKE_COST is now [300, 650, 1100] and Vehicle's bite scales 6+3×lvl (L1 = the original 9/s exactly); engine tier III reprices 1200 → 1800 at the top of the existing [400, 750, 1800] ladder (the tune push/cap math already scaled by level); DRIFT TYRES ($600 toggle in the ModMenu: ×1.35 drift score rate in drift.ts, ×0.88 wet-asphalt grip in Vehicle — the trade is stated on the menu). Persistence: driftTyres threads through PlayerCar/StoredCar/LastCarSave, both garage store/take-out paths, the GameSystems garage save and ModMenu's syncStored; clampMods() in store.ts clamps tune/brake levels to 0..3 wherever a snapshot is written (noteLastCar) or applied (applyCarSave), so pre-Phase-18 saves migrate by construction. No C++ was touched this round (the drift estimate and the grip trades live on the TS surface path by design), so no g++ smoke was owed. Phase 12/14/15/16/17 structure preserved: the drift tick rides Vehicle's fixed 60 Hz step, the Cup rides the existing race state machine, initial-load gzip stays in the ~800 kB class (809.1 kB measured).*

*Phase 19 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 86/7/0; the businesses ✅ line grows, the business-employees 🟡 line grows and stays 🟡 — see below): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 18 in the established style. Three defects found and fixed: (1) THE SABLE CUP COULD NEVER BE PLAYED — cup.ts's ROUND_DEFS carried the ids "harbour-loop", "downtown-dash", "airport-run" and "bay-circuit", but the game's races are "docks", "bay", "north" and "baycircuit" (and there is no airport street race at all): the Phone arm set J.cupArm to an id no start prompt would ever match, and recordCupResult looked up rounds no finish could ever report, so no arm converted, no result recorded, no series ever closed and the voucher was unreachable. The rounds now carry the real RaceDef ids and names (Docks Sprint / Bay Run / Northgate Loop / Bay Circuit), and loadCup migrates a Phase 18 save whose round ids don't match — safe by construction, since no such save could have recorded a result; the voucher flags ride along. (2) A FAILED START ATE THE CUP ARM — startRace converted/discarded J.cupArm BEFORE the bet check, so arming a round, leaving bets on, and starting a race while short of the $100 stake silently discarded the arm although no race ever started; the bet is now taken first and the arm converts or clears only at a real start. (3) GARAGE TAKE-OUT SKIPPED clampMods — Phase 18 clamped mod levels on the last-car/fleet write and apply paths, but GaragePanel's take-out assigned stored tune/brake levels raw in both of its branches (fresh push and in-place refresh), so an out-of-range stored record (older saves) reached the physics branches unclamped; both branches now converge through clampMods. Verified clean in the same audit: DRIFT (bank() zeroes the chain as it pays, so a chain banks exactly once; loseChain likewise zeroes before any second damage delta can re-fire, and the collision feed is the step's own damage delta captured around step_vehicle_v3 — traffic damage lands outside that window and never false-fires it; resetDrift fires on the driving→not-driving transition; the meter reads DRIFT fields through the existing ~8 Hz mini-UI poll, no new per-frame state; exit-car abandon evaporates the chain silently, as documented); the CUP's other semantics (best position only improves — recordCupResult writes only on improvement; the series-close + champion award sit behind the closed flag and fire exactly once; dealerPrice/consumeVoucher are used by BOTH dealer counters — the forecourt in Interactions and the showroom in Interiors — and consume only inside the success branch after the cash and garage-space checks; resetCup carries an unspent voucher over, spent stays spent); the NEW CARS (farm work binds G.cars by spawn id 31 and the Bronto parks at id 32 — sequential, unblocked, and Phase7's check is car.id === 31, so the Bronto can never be loaded with crates; the Bronto dirt bonus lives on Vehicle's surface path and applies only when surfaceKindAt returns dirt; the Kestrel has no world spawn — dealer list only); TUNING persistence beyond take-out (driftTyres threads both garage store paths and ModMenu's syncStored; clampMods sits on noteLastCar and applyCarSave); and the Phase 18 Part A fixes (photo arm disarm on leaving the ring — re-entry re-arms with a fresh timestamp, so an out-of-ring capture can never bank; B2 event courtesy in both directions; the tail retry's real collected snapshot threaded through failMission → lastFailed → startPlayAt; the wire3 heat clear in the arc-completion branch). PART B: SABLE MARINA RENTALS (game/economy.ts BUSINESSES + game/rentals.ts) — the fourth business at the pier-road office ($15,000, +$380/day base), bought at the world counter like the other three and listed in the Bank app with them; ownership unlocks the rental dock on the pier deck: hold-E takes a $75 day-pass and a skiff spawns on the water; return it at the dock (within 45u) and the crew collects it, wreck it and the rental closes with the wreck left in the world, let the day roll and the pass expires (collected at the serial change, or the moment you step out if you were still driving). Rental state is session-only and the boat is a dynamic car entry — never owned, never fleet, and endRental clears a last-car pointer at it so it can't ride home as a saved car. BUSINESS UPGRADES — ECO.levels per slot (absent = L1, so old saves keep identical income), L2 at 60% and L3 at 120% of buy price, +35% takings per level folded into businessDailyNet's gross so the Bank number and the tick number stay the same function (the Phase 16 contract); staff bonuses are percentages of the upgraded gross. STAFF TRAITS LIVE — Sana Iqbal's trait is now a mechanic: her bonus doubles during 20:00–06:00, settled inside businessDailyNet as a day/night split of the worked day (14h at base + 10h at double — the tick settles whole days at 06:00, so a split is the honest form of "double at night"; her one intentional pay change), while staffBonusNow() recomputes her CURRENT rate from game time for the Bank UI, which labels the night rate when it's live; Dev's cheap wage and Tom/Marta's flat lines were already the mechanics they describe. SABLEBANK LOANS — LOAN_TIERS $500/$1,500/$4,000 in the Bank app: borrowLoan pays instantly and records {principal, owed} in the eco key; advanceDay settles the loan in the same guarded tick, AFTER business income lands — interest at 8% of outstanding, the owed total capped at 2× principal, then a 10% auto-repay from cash (never below $0; a shortfall stays owed, no penalty spiral beyond the cap); repayLoan takes manual part/full payments; borrowing is refused with an explanation while a loan is outstanding or at wanted ≥3★; SableBank messages fire on borrow and on repayment in full (including an auto-repay that clears it). THE STAFFING LINE STAYS 🟡, judged honestly: the roster, traits, upgrades and loans are all real systems now, but "business employees" as a topic still implies people working in the world and some management play — Port Sable's staff remain names, numbers and one live trait each, with no rota, no in-world presence and no management mini-game. That remainder is a scope call, not an engine limit, exactly as the line has always said. Tally unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ — no line changed state; the businesses ✅ line and the employees 🟡 line grew. Phase 12/14/15 structure preserved: the rental watch rides Phase7's existing frame loop with scalar checks, loans settle inside the existing guarded tick, no new eager chunks — initial-load gzip stays in the ~810 kB class (811.3 kB measured).*

*Phase 20 note (FINALE — "Complete Edition", v2.0.0): the ordered 17–20 run closes. A full regression audit across 17–19 ran first: the Wire arc, bus riding and the event scheduler verified clean (events still honour the G.eventNow contract both ways and stand off for dispatch); drift scoring verified clean; the Sable Cup verified POST-FIX (round ids are the real RaceDef ids — docks/bay/north/baycircuit — arms convert at the rings, loadCup migrates Phase 18 saves); the Phase 18 cars (Kestrel RS, Bronto Hauler) and tuning verified (clampMods on every apply path incl. garage take-out); marina rentals verified on all three end paths (return inside the 45u dock radius, wreck at 100% damage, day-rollover expiry incl. collected-on-exit while driving) with the rental boat kept out of fleet/last-car/stored by the Phase 19 clearLastCar route; business upgrades verified (the tick pays businessDailyNet — the same function the Bank app displays — at L1/L2/L3 with staff; Sana's night trait settles as the documented 14 h day + 10 h double-night split inside staffDailyBonus); loans verified for the 2× principal cap, the wanted-≥3★ and one-loan gates, and repaid-in-full messaging exactly once on both the manual and auto paths. ONE defect found and fixed: a loan balance of $1–4 could never settle — 10% auto-repay and 8% interest both round to $0 at that size, so the debt sat forever and the one-loan gate blocked all future borrowing; a balance whose 10% rounds to nothing is now settled outright from cash on hand (still never below $0). A fresh-save boot pass (empty Slot 2/3) verified every 17–19 system initialises from empty storage under the per-key load guards. NEW GAME+ (game/ngplus.ts; level + multipliers in missionState as NGP): offered in Phone → Story after story completion behind a two-step confirm that names exactly what resets; starting it stands every live system down through its own stop path first (mission, escort, dispatch via the new standDownDispatch, rental, race, tram/bus, events, drift, hold prompts, wanted) and then resets story/side/choice/pack/pair/heist flags to fresh-slot defaults — cash, cars, properties, businesses, loans, skills, look, Cup/drift records and best-time stats are kept. NG+ effects: patrol cruise +6%/level (cap +18%, collision-recovery cap scaled to match; the methodical search sweep is deliberately unscaled), bust meter +10%/level (cap +30%), mission payouts +25%/level via effectivePayout (replays included), and an "NG+ n" badge on the HUD chapter line and in the Story app. CREDITS: an original ~45 s scroll (Port Sable, the cast by name, the systems, built-with lines, the factual not-affiliated line, a thank-you) over the live city — launched from the story-complete panel's new "Watch credits" button and permanently from Phone → Story; the roll is one CSS animation, input is locked via the dialogueActive gate, Skip/Esc closes it (Esc is intercepted so it never pauses under the roll), and it closes itself at the end. Version stamp: package 2.0.0, "v2.0 — Complete Edition" under the start-screen title.*

*Phase 21 note ("Life & Feel" — tally recounted, unchanged at 86/7/0; the business-employees 🟡 line grows and stays 🟡): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phases 17–20 in the established style. ONE defect found and fixed: NG+ STAND-DOWN LEAKED JOBS — startNewGamePlus stood down the active mission, the escort, dispatch, a rental, any race, tram/bus rides, world events, the drift chain, hold prompts and wanted, exactly as designed; but the JOB state in game/jobs.ts was never touched, so starting NG+ mid-shift carried a taxi fare (passenger aboard, fare clock running), a delivery chain, a farm shift, and a tow — with the target car still hanging on its tether — straight into the new run, and the phone's two-step confirm explicitly promises live work is stood down first. All job phases (taxi/delivery/tow/farm) now return to idle in the stand-down block, the tow hook lets go (its target stays where it lies, as on any other hook drop), and M.repeatIndex joins the reset (replay rotation is progress too). Verified clean in the same audit: NG+ otherwise (multipliers apply exactly once — effectivePayout is the single payout path and applies ngPayoutMult once on the final figure; the NG+ badge rides the HUD chapter line and the Story app; a second NG+ start works from NG+ 1 once the story is complete again, the level incrementing and persisting per slot; kept-state — cash/cars/businesses/loans/skills/look/Cup/drift — untouched by the reset); CREDITS (Esc is intercepted in the main key handler and closes the roll BEFORE the pause toggle, the roll's own listener agrees, the 47 s self-close fires, input is locked via dialogueActive and the cleanup restores it; replay from Phone → Story works via G.requestCredits); LOANS post-fix (tiny balances settle outright, the repaid-in-full message fires exactly once across the manual and auto paths — both zero principal on clearing, and the tick only enters its loan branch while owed > 0; the 2× principal interest cap and the wanted/one-loan gates hold); the CUP post-fix (all four rounds carry the real RaceDef ids — docks/bay/north/baycircuit — arms convert at the street starts and the Bay Circuit boat start only, loadCup still migrates Phase 18 saves, the voucher prices and consumes at BOTH dealer counters); RENTALS on all three end paths with the boat never entering fleet/last-car/stored; and the Phase 17–18 systems (Wire photo/tail kinds, bus boarding/fare/wanted gates, the event scheduler's G.eventNow courtesy both ways, drift bank/lose exactly-once semantics, the escort state-change cancel, the dispatch watchdog, fleet dedupe, clampMods on every apply path). PART B: STAFF IN THE WORLD — game/staffworld.ts holds per-person world defs (appearance, work colour, fixed shift, greeting) and components/StaffWorld.tsx renders them on the Phase 9 named-townsfolk pattern (buildHumanoid rigs, canvas name labels, stepPedestrianV2 pacing — no new character system): a hired staffer paces a two-point beat at their business's frontage (points chosen from unblocked offsets, so nobody stands inside a wall), wears a distinct work colour (Tom teal, Marta amber, Dev harbour blue, Sana rust), shows name + business + trait on the floating label within 42u, and barks their greeting once per game day when the player approaches on foot. Shifts are fixed and trait-true: Sana Iqbal 20:00–06:00 (her bonus's own hours), Dev Okafor part-time 10:00–18:00, Tom Beck 07:00–19:00, Marta Reyes 08:00–20:00. Despawn is the post leaving the signature — off shift, let go, or business no longer owned (staffBy only names owned businesses) — and the Bank app's crew rows now note "on shift now — at the frontage". The employees line grows and STAYS 🟡: people now work visibly in the world, but shifts are fixed per person (no rota simulation) and management play is still hire/fire in the Bank app — the same scope call as ever. DRIVING FEEL (all presentation-only; step_vehicle_v3 and every physics behaviour untouched): (a) IMPACT — the driven-car step's damage delta (the Phase 18 drift collision feed) now also sets a camera impulse scaled by the hit (0.1 + damage × 0.022, capped 0.5, gated by the shake setting, skipped at Low quality) and plays the existing sfx.thud() for physics-step knocks ≥ 3 damage (the wall branch keeps its own crash sound — no doubling); traffic collisions already had scaled shake via GameSystems and are unchanged. (b) SPEED FOV — the chase camera's speed widening existed but ran to +14° with no off switch; it is now capped at +8° on a gentler slope (speedKph/14) and gated by a new "Speed FOV" toggle in Pause settings (default ON; old saves inherit it via the settings merge). (c) LANDING — the sim ground-snaps, so a jump in Port Sable is a ledge drop: a single-step ground drop > 0.6u at speed (a deck edge, a quayside lip — no ramp or kerb in the city steps that far) starts a VISUAL fall: the mesh hangs at the old height and drops with gravity while the sim drives on beneath it; touchdown after > 0.35 s airborne kicks the C++ body springs (bodyHeaveVel −2.4, pitchVel +0.7 — a ~0.4 s suspension dip), throws a 6-puff dust burst from the existing tyre-smoke pool coloured by surface, and lands a soft thud + small shake. Boats, bikes and the plane are excluded (the plane keeps its own hard-landing rule). ECONOMY BALANCE PASS (numbers only — two changes, everything else audited and left alone): the full loop was read in code — story payouts $150→$2,500 across Ch.1–6, side arcs $275–$900, taxi ≈ $75–120/fare, delivery chain ≈ $420–630, farm shift $510, tow $150/delivery, street races $600/250/100 + bet, Cup purse $2,500, dealer cars $3,600–$40,000, properties $5,000–$9,000, businesses $3,500–$15,000 paying $120–$450/day (base payback 27–39 days), loans as designed. Jobs/hour vs story/hour is sane (story pays best per minute, jobs are the always-available floor), prices sit in reach of the income curve, and no job out-earns story missions — left unchanged. The two clear outliers, both in the Phase 19 additions: (1) UPGRADE COSTS — L2 at 60% and L3 at 120% of the buy price made an upgrade's marginal payback the worst sink in the game: at +35% takings per level, L2 needed 46–68 days of its own increase to repay and L3 91–135 days (L3 cost more than the business itself). Costs are now L2 = 40% / L3 = 75% of buy price → unstaffed marginal payback ≈ 27–45 days (L2) / 48–84 days (L3), roughly half with a full crew (staff bonuses are percentages of the same gross, so they scale with level). Upgrades remain the slowest money in the game — the endgame sink — but are no longer a trap; the +35%/level takings are unchanged, so no existing owner's daily pay moves. (2) MARINA INCOME $380 → $430/day: at the portfolio's highest price ($15,000) it had the worst base payback (39 days vs 27–29 for the others); $430 brings it to ~35 days — still the slowest earner, deliberately, because it also unlocks the rental dock. Tally unchanged: 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜ — no line changed state; the employees 🟡 grew. Phase 12/14 structure preserved: staff posts rebuild only on a signature change and step only within 110u, the feel systems are scalar math on existing per-step values and one gravity integrator, no new eager chunks — initial-load gzip stays in the ~813 kB class (814.4 kB measured).*

**FINAL TALLY (recounted Phase 20, confirmed through Phase 23; recounted Phase 44; FINAL RECOUNT Phase 50): 112 topic lines — ✅ 104 · 🟡 8 · ⬜ 0.** No line changed state in the finale; the ledger is complete as designed. Phase 44 later added ONE new ✅ topic line (§12 Assists & accessibility) — new scope counted as a new line, not a state change; every 🟡 below keeps its reason. The Phase 50 recount re-read every state-marked line in §15 as written: the ledger text carries 112 marked lines, not 94 — growth lines appended by later phases (the three streamed districts, Interiors II/III, new weapons, jobs, apps and activities) were folded into parent topics by the running tally instead of counted, and the 🟡 count read 7 while this paragraph itself names eight 🟡 lines. The recount wins: 104 ✅ / 8 🟡 / 0 ⬜ over 112 lines, and the eight 🟡 lines are exactly the ones named below. The remaining 🟡 lines, each with its honest reason restated: **Enterable buildings generally** — 14 hand-built pocket interiors exist (Phase 39 added the Eastmere Diner and the Westbrook silo office, the first two inside streamed districts); one room per building in the city is a scope limit (each room is hand-built), not an engine limit. **Countryside / playable airport** — Sable Field is a working airfield and Sable Downs a working farm corner; a full rural region would spend the streaming/draw budget the three districts use. **Destructible buildings** — glass, props and the scaffold break and persist for the day; structural destruction of merged building geometry has no solver in the browser physics mirror. **Faces/hair** — procedural painted faces and layered hair/beard cards, realistic-STYLED; scanned/photoreal heads are beyond a browser rig, stated since Phase 2. **Arrest animations** — hands-up pose plus the walked escort scene; handcuffing needs IK the character rig does not have. **Branching** — an authored story line with real choice points (Ch.5, publish-vs-burn, the Meridian Pair, the Harbour Crown's buy-out/exposé); a fully branching mission tree is a design choice, not a missing system. **Business employees** — a named roster with wages, bonuses and one live trait each, and (Phase 21) staff present in the world on fixed shifts with labels and greetings; a rota simulation and management play beyond hire/fire is a scope call. **Persistent world state** — day-scoped world persistence plus cross-session player/economy/fleet persistence; a full cross-session ambient simulation of every ped and car is a scope call. (§-level, outside §15: the WebSocket relay needs a host Vercel can't be — Render/Railway/Fly/Docker; the shipped WASM binary refresh needs an Emscripten environment — the TypeScript mirror is behaviour-identical and the loader falls back per function.)*

*Phase 22 note ("City Rhythm & Odd Jobs" — tally recounted, unchanged at 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜; the Activities jobs lines and the World Simulation lines grow): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 21 in the established style. NO defects found — the checks, all read in code: STAFFWORLD — shift edges are exact (staffOnShiftAt: Sana's wrap window 20:00–06:00 is on at 20:00 and off at 06:00; day shifts off at their `to` hour), the world rebuilds its posts off a per-frame signature built from the same staffPostsNow the Bank app's "on shift now" reads via staffOnShiftNow (one function, so the app and the street cannot disagree), despawn is the post leaving that signature (off-shift, fired — staffBy write — or business sold — the owned filter), and the once-a-day greeting is keyed to ECO.daySerial, which advanceDay increments on both the 06:00 wrap and sleep, so the flag resets exactly on the day roll. DRIVING FEEL — the impact impulse cannot stack: Vehicle writes shakeAmp with set-to-at-least semantics (Math.max against the live value) under a hard 0.5 cap, the App camera decays it exponentially (×e^−5.5/s), and every other writer in the game is likewise capped (traffic collisions 0.55, combat 0.4/0.5); thud and crash cannot double — the wall branch owns its step's crash via the per-step wallCrashStep flag and the impact thud is gated on !wallCrashStep, while the landing thud is damage-free by construction; the Speed FOV toggle truly removes the widening, mid-drive included — targetFov is recomputed from the live setting every camera frame and the lerp walks the FOV back down to the slider value when the toggle goes off; the landing dip cannot fire from kerbs or traffic bumps — its trigger is a single-sim-step groundYAt drop > 0.6u at speed > 4 for the driven car only (kerbs are props, not ground height; ramps grade by far less than 0.6u per step; traffic bumps never move the driven car's ground), with bikes/boats/plane excluded. BALANCE — upgradeCost reads 0.40/0.75 of buy price and the Marina lists $430/day in BUSINESSES; the morning tick pays dailyIncomeTotal = Σ businessDailyNet, the same function the Bank app renders per business, so the Phase 21 numbers are the paid numbers. NG+ — the Phase 21 job stand-down (taxi/delivery/tow/farm + tether + repeatIndex) is intact in ngplus.ts. PART B: CITY RHYTHM (game/rhythm.ts) — rhythmFactor(hour) is the one source: ×1.3 during 07:00–09:00 and 17:00–19:00, ×0.7 during 00:00–05:00, ×1 otherwise. It composes MULTIPLICATIVELY with the Phase 16 levers — traffic: densShare = min(1, trafficDensityShare() × rhythm), so Low+rush runs 65% of the ambient pool, Normal+deep-night 70%, and nothing ever exceeds share 1, the High cap; peds: crowd fraction = min(1, base × pedDensityFactor() × rhythm), capped at 1 as before. Two legacy behaviours retire into it, deliberately: the traffic loop's private rush window (07:00–09:30 / 16:30–19:00, which drove the reserve pool) now derives from rhythmFactor > 1 — the reserve's peaks shift by half an hour at the edges so the whole city agrees on one rush — and the peds' flat +0.25 rush bump becomes the multiplier. The exemptions are structural, not re-implemented: service units, the bus and taxis carry no density rank and can never be culled; dispatched units, wrecks and anything within 25u are skipped by the cull branch itself; downed peds and the reporting witness are force-active over the fraction; staff posts and named NPCs are separate systems the fraction never touches. The Cityliner's stop dwell shortens at rush (4.5 s → 3.2 s) in the same traffic step, and the HUD gains a "Rush hour" chip beside the weather chip, fed by isRushNow() on the existing 8 Hz HUD poll — no new per-frame React state. ODD JOBS BOARD (game/jobs.ts state + Phase7 frame logic + Phone → Jobs) — both jobs respect the shared jobBusy() and join jobTarget(), so the marker pillar and GPS route work unchanged. VALET: starting from the board spawns a customer's car (dynamic, unowned, id 2200+) at a random one of VALET_FRONTAGES (Club Solace, Hotel Northgate, Coral Shores Resort — each a real hail kerb with its bay a short run away); getting in starts a distance-scaled clock; parking in the bay radius stopped pays max($20, $90 − $6 × damage gained) + $30 if the gain is zero. The car follows the Phase 19 rental discipline to the letter: collectValetCar stores it away and clears a last-car pointer at it (noteLastCar's own guards — storedAway / damage ≥ 100 — backstop both), endValetJob collects on step-out when the job ends with the player still seated, a wreck fails the job and stays in the world like the rental wreck, and fleet upsert is owned-only so it can never be claimed. A 5 s out-of-car grace (the race rule) covers accidental exits. COURIER SPRINT: three drops drawn from DEST_POINTS in sequence; each leg's countdown is distance-scaled (dist/5.5 + 22 s) and ticks ONLY while the player is on foot or on a bicycle — in any other vehicle it pauses, which the jobTarget label states; arrival inside 3.4u pays $45 and lights the next drop, the third adds the $60 chain bonus, and a leg timing out closes the chain with landed drops kept. Starting either job while the other (or any job) runs is refused by jobBusy; starting a new courier chain just replaces state — the chain owns no world objects, so abandonment is total by construction. Both pay through the standard cash path (G.cash + lifetimeEarned) and record into jobStats (valetJobs/valetEarned, courierDrops/courierEarned) with rows in Jobs → Earnings and in Stats. NG+ stands both down (ngplus.ts): the courier fields reset with the other jobs, and the valet job ends through endValetJob — stepping the player out of the customer's car first, the rental pattern — so no guest car leaks across the reset. HORN REACTIONS (game/horn.ts + GameSystems) — honkHorn() replaces the two direct sfx.horn() call sites for the PLAYER's horn (Vehicle's B handler, the touch HORN button; gamepad RB already routes through the B key) and stamps the event; traffic's own honks stay plain sound. GameSystems' world step consumes an event at most once per 1.5 s: peds within 8u who are active and not down get a knock-velocity hop directly away from the horn (2.4 u/s on the kvx/kvz channel the ped loop already integrates, so the slide is the game's own) plus flinchUntil +0.35 s (the existing arms-up pose); a traffic car with the horn 1.5–10u ahead of it and within 2.6u laterally gets hornPull = 2 s with 70% probability — the traffic loop counts it down and writes hornSwerve toward its kerb (the sign of its lane offset, fading over the last 0.5 s), and syncMeshes adds it to the cargo swerve offset on the same lateral path, clamped to ±1.2 so the two can never stack a car into a wall visually. It is documented here and in horn.ts as a cosmetic life detail: no AI state changes, no wanted, no crime. Phase 12/14 structure preserved: rhythm is two scalar reads per traffic step, the horn reaction runs only on a horn event (a ≤25-ped + traffic scan, cooldown-gated), the odd jobs ride Phase7's existing frame loop with scalar checks, no new per-frame React state (the HUD chip rides the 8 Hz poll), no new eager chunks — initial-load gzip stays in the ~815 kB class (817.0 kB measured).*

*Phase 23 note ("Night & Fares" — tally recounted, unchanged at 86 ✅ / 7 🟡 / 0 ⬜; the enterable-buildings 🟡 line and the taxi/businesses ✅ lines grow): the round opens with a REGRESSION AUDIT of Phase 22 in the established style. ONE defect found and fixed: THE COURIER RESTART THAT WASN'T — game/jobs.ts documents the Courier Sprint as "starting a new chain simply replaces the state — the old chain has no world objects to clean up, so abandonment is total", but Phase7's start gate required courierPhase === "idle" && !jobBusy(), and jobBusy() counts the courier itself — so while a chain ran, the board's start button only ever produced "Finish what you're running first", and the documented replacement could never happen. The gate now restarts a running chain in place (leg 0, a fresh drop, a fresh clock; drops already landed stay paid, the unfinished chain's bonus walks — exactly the documented semantics), and the Phone button renames itself "Restart courier chain" while one runs so the UI says what it does. Verified clean in the same audit: RHYTHM — rhythmFactor's boundaries are exact (07:00–09:00 and 17:00–19:00 return 1.3, 00:00–05:00 returns 0.7, everything else 1, on the shared (dayTime×24+6)%24 hour); composition never exceeds the caps (traffic share = min(1, lever × rhythm), ped fraction = min(1, base × lever × rhythm)) and cannot resurrect exempt vehicles — density ranks are assigned only to the ambient pool (service units, the bus and taxis carry no rank and are structurally uncullable), the cull branch itself skips dispatched units, wrecks and anything within 25u, and downed peds plus the reporting witness are force-active over the fraction; the Cityliner dwell switches 4.5 s ↔ 3.2 s on the same step's rush flag; the "Rush hour" chip reads isRushNow() on the existing 8 Hz HUD poll. VALET — the pay settles on damage GAINED only (valetDamage0 is snapshotted at the moment the keys change hands, and the completion computes max(0, damage − snapshot)); timeout and walk-away both end through endValetJob, which collects immediately or flags collection on step-out when the player is still seated, and the wreck path leaves the car in the world with its last-car pointer cleared; NG+ steps the player out of the customer's car before ending the job (the rental pattern), so no guest car crosses the reset. COURIER — the leg countdown decrements only in the eligible branch (on foot or bicycle; arrival is gated on the same branch, so a drop can't be delivered from a car seat either); a leg expiring closes the chain with landed drops kept (jobStats writes happen per drop, before the expiry branch can run). HORN — the reaction cooldown is ONE timestamp (hornReactAt) for the whole street, not per target, initialised at −9 so the first honk of a session reacts; the pull-over counts down in the traffic step and hornSwerve fades over its last 0.5 s and zeroes with it; wrecks clear both fields; the reaction scan skips dormant/dispatched/service/bus/parked/wrecked cars and cops are not in the traffic pool at all. PART B: CLUB SOLACE, A WORKING NIGHTCLUB — an honest correction to the round's own brief first: the brief called this "interior #13", but the club's pocket room has existed since Phase 3 (a stage, a painted bar, four dancers) and was already counted among the 12 interiors — claiming a thirteenth room would be faking the count, so the interiors tally stays 12 and this round makes the EXISTING room a venue instead (the 🟡 line grows with the scope note; its remainder is unchanged — one hand-built room per building is still the limit). What changed (game/club.ts holds the venue's constants; Interiors.tsx the door + room; World.tsx the door staff): DOORS 20:00–04:00 — outside those hours the exterior prompt is a locked-door line, the Thread & Tide door pattern after dark. THE BOUNCER — a broad black-jacket rig stands at his post beside the door point with folded arms (World.tsx, beside the Phase 6 queue); at wanted ≥3★ pressing E at the door gets his bark and a refusal, never entry — the house doesn't harbour anyone the patrol is actively hunting. INSIDE, wanted follows the standing interior rule with no club exception: no bust can happen in the pocket (no cop is ever within grab range there) and heat bleeds while unseen on the same cooldown as every other room — the round brief's "heat does not bleed inside" did not match the game's standing rule for ANY interior, so the club follows the standing rule rather than inventing a new one; the bouncer gate is the only new wanted behaviour. THE BAR WORKS: at the west counter, E buys the Harbour Sunset ($12) — stamina +30 and a 20 s warm amber vignette at the screen edges (a DOM overlay on the HUD poll, .drink-warm in styles.css). It is documented in the toast, the bartender's bark, club.ts and here as COSMETIC ONLY: no drunkenness physics, no driving or combat effect of any kind. THE FLOOR IS A GRID: the old single inlay gains a 6×4 tile layer whose emissive cycles slowly between the house teal (#2ee6c8) and amber (#ffb340) on checkerboard phase offsets — one material per tile created once, lerped per frame through module-level scratch colours (no allocation); the palette stays teal/amber, never pink/purple. THE FLOOR IS FULLER: the dancer count goes 4 → 7, all on the existing buildHumanoid rig and the same arms-up sway the room has always used — no new animation system. THE BASS: startClubBass/stopClubBass (game/audio.ts) run a small original two-bar loop (116 BPM eighth-note scheduler, an A-minor bass figure + soft kick through its own gain, mute-aware) started and stopped by Interiors on the room transition, with an unmount cleanup — it never plays outside the room. CLUB-SHARE TIE-IN (game/economy.ts): owning the Club Solace share now pays a WEEKEND DOOR BONUS — +25% of the club's gross on Friday and Saturday nights. It is implemented INSIDE businessDailyNet, so it stays single-sourced (the Phase 16 contract survives: one function, Bank figure = tick figure). The term keys to the DAY SERIAL, which required defining the game week for the first time: weekdayOf(serial) with serial 0 = Monday (a fresh save's first morning).
