PAD: triangle tones with a 0.9 s attack through a lowpass, chord data copied from the theme's own BARS (Fmaj7 | Dm9 | Bbmaj7 | Cmaj7 — same family as "Port Sable Sunset", slower pulse); a tension latch read at bar lines swaps the progression to TENSE_BARS (Dm9 | Bbmaj7 | Gm9 | Am7 — the relative-minor side of the same key) while G.wanted ≥ 3, so the voicing never changes mid-bar. PLUCK: two steps a bar (3 and 10), chord tones +12 cycled per bar, triangle plucks; its target level is clamp01(G.nightFactor) — the same value the world lighting integrates — so the layer breathes with dusk and dawn instead of switching. PULSE: a pitch-dropping sine thump at the chord root +24 and a highpassed noise tick, both into the pulse bus; at wanted 3–4 the thump lands on beats (steps %4) with a tick at %8==4; at 5★+ the thump doubles to 8ths (%2) and ticks fill %4==2 — quickening is a rate change, not a volume trick. FADES: each layer keeps a JS level eased toward its target at dt/2 s per tick (≈2 s full fade) and the layer gain is written from the level every tick; notes are scheduled only while their level > 0.02, so a gated score schedules nothing and a returning layer fades in under notes already being scheduled at low gain. PRIORITY: the tick's gate is `!settings.ambientScore || TUNES_STATE.playing || tunesRoomHold() !== null` — TUNES_STATE.playing covers a sounding track (a suspended track implies a room hold, which the third clause catches), and tunesRoomHold() is a new read-only export of tunes.ts' single hold slot: the score consults the same slot the phone obeys, it does not keep a copy. App.tsx calls scoreStart() inside beginPlay beside resumeTunes() — the score cannot sound on the start screen (the theme owns it, started by the splash gesture handler as always), and since `started` is one-way (no return-to-title exists in the flow), no stop path is needed. MISSIONS: a census of every music start in the client (startMenuTheme / startClubBass / playTune / startLoop) shows missions own NO music — mission feedback is sfx stings (complete/fail/star) — so the score continues under missions, documented here and in the module header rather than inventing a mission duck. MUTE: the score's bus is a musicBus() gain re-synced by syncMusicBus on every tick — the N key lands within ~90 ms, the identical treatment Tunes gets; toggleMute needs no new knowledge of it. COST, counted: 4 persistent gain nodes (bus + pad + pluck + pulse), one 90 ms interval, transient voices ≤ 4 pad tones + 2 plucks + ≤8 pulse voices per bar, all stopped at envelope end in the house idiom. SETTING: G.settings.ambientScore (default true) in the settings object — per-slot via the existing load merge (old saves gain it as true), Phone → Settings gains a Music section with the toggle and an honest tip (priority order named; Off = Phase 45 audio). NUMBERS: build PASS — index JS 253.36 → 254.25 kB gzip, initial total 629.03 → ≈629.92 kB (+0.14%); posterData still its own lazy chunk (236.14), districts untouched (Eastmere 3.20, Westbrook 2.94, Northreach 2.93). Placement audit ALL PLACEMENTS CLEAR (no world changes); pytest 48/48 re-run (server untouched). No C++ touched.*

*Phase 45 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 87/7/0 over 94 topic lines; the §15 Smoothness line grows, no line changed state): PERFORMANCE ROUND 2. PART A — PHASE 44 REGRESSION AUDIT. ONE defect found and fixed; the rest clean. DEFECT — Drive Assist Full's camera auto-centre ignored the assist's own documented scope. The Settings help (Phone) says Full "eases the camera back behind your car", store.ts documents driveAssist as road vehicles only, and Vehicle's steering smoothing honours that with a boat/plane exclusion — but CameraRig's auto-centre block (App.tsx) gated only on G.driving ∧ activeCarId ∧ !orbit ∧ !G.interior, and boats and the plane ARE G.cars entries with G.driving set, so the block ran for them. Behind a boat it was merely off-label; behind the plane it actively fought the player, because a banking turn swings state.heading continuously and the azimuth chase (≤0.55 rad/s toward behind-the-heading) trailed every manoeuvre. Fix: the block now computes roadVehicle = kind ∉ {boat, skiff, plane} — the same exclusion list as the smoothing — before easing. CLEAN, verified in code: (1) STEERING RESET — assistSteerRef zeroes on the drive-start edge (wasDriving) and tracks the raw input on every frame the assist is off or the vehicle is a boat/plane, so a stale smoothed value cannot survive an exit, a toggle or a vehicle swap. (2) CAMERA EXCLUSIONS COMPLETE — the spawn sweep, the aim camera and the FP/photo camera all return before the auto-centre block; interiors and the menu orbit gate it off; the tram and bus rides never set G.driving (boarding requires !G.driving and the ride state is separate), so no ride camera can reach it. (3) AIM MAGNETISM COST — the block sits inside fire(), gated on G.aiming ∧ setting ≠ off; it scans the bounded ped pool once per shot and samples isBlocked every ~1.2u only for candidates inside the cone and weapon range, breaking at the first blocked sample. Per-shot, bounded, and zero when not aiming. (4) HUDPALETTE OFF PATH — hudCanvasPalette returns the theme's own colours + the map's standing literals; hudDomPalette returns all-null; every DOM caller applies `pal.x ? { … } : undefined`, and the properties touched are colour-only, so the Off path cannot shift layout. (5) LIGHTNING CONSUMERS vs THE 0.3 PEAK — census: Weather's bolt opacity (×0.95, visibility floor 0.03), GameSystems' ambient intensity (+1.7×), scene background and fog scalars (+0.35×). All are multiplicative/additive scalings of the value; nothing divides by G.lightning, thresholds it high, or feeds a damage system (lightning has been spectacle-only since Phase 36), so the reduced-flash peak of 0.3 simply scales the whole spectacle down and the fixed decay shortens it — no consumer misbehaves. (6) SETTINGS MERGE — store.ts loads harborfall-settings as { ...defaults, ...saved }, so a pre-Phase-44 save gains driveAssist/aimAssist/colorblindHud/reducedFlash at their defaults on first load. PART B — MEASURE, THEN ACT. BASELINE (vite's own gzip accounting, this tree before changes): index.html 0.66 + CSS 11.01 + index JS 490.54 + three 178.23 + react-three 185.77 = 866.21 kB initial; lazy: cc0Data 959.18, Phone 17.48, Eastmere 3.20, Westbrook 2.94, Northreach 2.93, GaragePanel 1.75, ModMenu 1.53, CharacterSelect 0.73. PROFILE FINDINGS: the fixed-step sim (GameSystems stepSim at 60 Hz) allocates only on events (mission objective objects, collect Sets) — there is no per-tick Vector3/array churn to remove (Player/Vehicle already use module scratch vectors; the one per-frame-looking filter/map block in GameSystems is the 1 Hz depenetration sweep); canvas textures are disciplined (facades/signs ≤512, faces 128, CC0 capped 768/512 by tier since Phase 14) — the single outlier is the PORT SABLE hillside sign at 2048×320 (~2.6 MB GPU, one texture, read from across the city) and it stays; each palm crown runs its own useFrame distance check (~40 subscriptions × ~10 flops — real but negligible, and consolidating it would risk the Phase 12 palm rebuild for ~µs). THE ONE BIG WIN: posterData.ts — 317,797 bytes of base64 JPEG key-art (9 panels) statically imported by App.tsx and used ONLY on the splash/poster start screen, contributing ~237 kB gzip to the initial bundle. ACTED: (1) POSTER CHUNK SPLIT — App now loads the panels through a cached dynamic import (the cc0Textures pattern); the start screen renders its shade, title and Play button immediately over the stage-1 city and the panels fade in when the chunk lands; the slideshow timer guards on a non-empty set. Initial load: index JS 490.54 → 253.36 kB gzip; new lazy posterData chunk 236.14 kB gzip; INITIAL TOTAL 866.21 → 629.03 kB (−237.2 kB, −27.4%). The poster chunk still downloads on the start screen — it is off the critical path, not off the network, stated plainly. (2) THE LOW PRESET, made real (Low existed since Phase 5 with composer off / no normal maps / flat water / CC0 off; this round audits it and adds the missing teeth): SHADOWS OFF — GameSystems' per-frame sun write is now `castShadow = settings.shadows && quality !== "low"`, so on Low no shadow map is allocated or rendered at all (the Phase 25 every-3rd-frame re-render at Low is moot — there is nothing to re-render; the needsUpdate flag may still be set by the snap block but three's shadow pass skips non-casting lights, so it renders nothing). The player's Shadows toggle still rules on Medium/High. DPR CAP 1.25 → 1.0 (game/perf.ts tierDprCap) — Low renders at native resolution at most, ~36% fewer fragments than 1.25; PerfGovernor already clamps its ladder index to the tier cap every window, so the preset IS the ceiling and the governor cannot fight it (verified at the governor's capIdx clamp). DENSITY CEILINGS — trafficDensityShare/pedDensityFactor in perf.ts now take min(lever, Low values) when the tier is Low (traffic ≤ 0.5 share, crowd ≤ ×0.5) through the same single-source functions every reader already calls; on Medium/High the formulas are the pre-Phase-45 ones verbatim. WILDLIFE HALF RATE — Wildlife's ambient block (pod + gulls + splashes) runs every other frame on Low with the summed dt; everything in it integrates from dt or reads the clock, so behaviour is identical sampled at ~30 Hz (POD_MEMBERS is at most one skipped frame stale for the photo challenges). COMPOSER AT LOW — already fully unmounted (gates.composer), and SSAO/SSR/planar water are High-only gates, so there was NO pass left to remove at Low; claimed as audited, not as a change. The harbour flock is nine cones in one group with one transform write per frame — left alone, stated. (3) HELP TEXT — the pause-menu Graphics note and the Phone → Settings graphics section now describe Low as the weak-phone preset with its trades named (no shadows = a flatter-looking city), and the governor's never-above-the-cap rule. AFTER TABLE (same accounting): index.html 0.66 + CSS 11.01 + index JS 253.36 + three 178.23 + react-three 185.77 = 629.03 kB initial (−27.4%); lazy chunks unchanged except Phone 17.48 → 17.63 (the help text) and the new posterData 236.14; streamed districts untouched (Eastmere 3.20, Westbrook 2.94, Northreach 2.94). NOT MEASURED, NOT CLAIMED: no FPS figure appears in this note — the changes bound the worst cases (fill rate, shadow pass, entity counts, download size) and the HUD FPS counter on the player's own device is the only honest verdict. VERIFICATION: build PASS; placement audit ALL PLACEMENTS CLEAR (no world changes); pytest 48/48 re-run (server untouched). No C++ touched.*

*Phase 49 note (this round — tally recounted, unchanged at 87/7/0 over 94 topic lines; no line changed state — this round hardened what the lines already claim): REGRESSION MARATHON — the combined Phases 26–48 tree audited as one surface for the first time. PART A — PHASE 48 (THE HARBOUR CROWN) AUDIT, all verified clean in code. (1) ESCROW EXACTLY-ONCE: App.tsx's resolveChoice charges the $40,000 once, guarded by !M.crownPaid, and the same synchronous handler sets crownPaid and calls persistMission() — cash and flags land atomically in the slot's mission record, so a crash/reload between charge and persist cannot exist; a failed attempt re-offers the locked branch and the guard refuses a second charge. The offer gate cannot stale: the choice panel is presented only when G.cash ≥ 40,000 at presentation and the player sits in dialogue UI (cannot spend) until the click. (2) DIVIDEND SHOWN = PAID: businessDailyNet applies the ×1.1 LAST, gated on crownBuyoutLive() (buyout ∧ crownDone ∧ crownPaid); the Bank's per-business rows, its daily total and the morning tick's dailyIncomeTotal all call that one function — no second computation exists anywhere (grep census in B.3). One timing note, not a defect: completing crown3 mid-day applies the dividend to that whole day's settlement — the Bank display applies it identically to today's figure, so shown = paid still holds exactly. (3) EXPOSÉ SERIES EXACTLY-ONCE: crownDayTick runs only inside advanceDay's serial guard AND no-ops unless serial > SERIES.lastSerial; the fired serial is persisted (harborfall-crownseries) synchronously BEFORE the headline is pushed, so a reload mid-series replays nothing; ECO.daySerial never rewinds (not even across NG+), so lastSerial monotonicity is structural. armExposeSeries deliberately does not reset lastSerial — safe for the same reason. (4) SOVEREIGN DELIVERY: the garage path (room in the 6-slot list → storedCars + persist, take out with G at either garage) and the reload path (stored cars / garage snapshot are ordinary save data) verified; NG+ resets crownCarDelivered while cars persist, and a second completion's existence checks find the first car — no duplicate. ONE REAL HOLE in this story was found by the marathon (B.1/B.4 below) and fixed. (5) WANTED CAP vs EVIDENCE FLOOR: crown2's per-frame clamp caps G.wanted at 3; the Phase 29 floor is min(3, ⌊evidence/25⌋) — floor and cap meet AT 3 and can never fight (the floor can never demand more than the cap allows); the escape step's wanted-0 demand is reachable because the floor applies on the next REPORTED crime, not continuously. crown4's forceWanted 4 is cleared in the completion fallout (the wire3 precedent); on failure it decays through the normal heat search path (11 s + evidence × 0.08) — no timer is left pinned. PART B — THE MARATHON. B.1 PERSISTENCE CENSUS: 65 harborfall-* literals in client/src; two are window-event names, not storage (harborfall-clothing-changed, harborfall-postcards-changed), and harborfall-slots-meta is the slot layer's own exempt meta key. Every real key routes through the Phase 14 slot-prefix layer — saveslots.ts patches Storage.prototype; the only raw localStorage access in the codebase is saveslots' own meta handling; there is no sessionStorage or IndexedDB anywhere. Every load is guarded: store.ts/missionState.ts load through a per-key try/catch loadKey, and the module-level loads (economy incl. rentals + bizEvents, heat, postcards, dayevents, crown series, characters) validate field by field with defaults — corrupt or legacy data degrades to defaults, never a crash. NG+ decisions were enumerated key by key against ngplus.ts; the keys it never named are now given explicit decisions in a Phase 49 census comment in ngplus.ts (comment only): KEEPS by day-serial design (eco incl. bizEvents, dayevents, crownseries — an armed exposé finishes printing in the new run; gym/destruct/wrecks/scaffold day sets), KEEPS as career/profile records (jobstats, postcards, cup, slide, invest), KEEPS as continuing life (msgs/news/social, clothing, tunes), KEEPS as device/profile preferences (keybinds, gyro, theme, character, mproom, intro, tram-news), RESETS (heat via resetHeat, already coded). TWO DEFECTS FOUND HERE. (a) THE MULTIPLAYER NAME WAS A DEAD KEY: leaderboard.ts's playerName() read harborfall-mp-name — a key NOTHING in the codebase ever writes — so every leaderboard/economy service post went out as "Rayan Kade" regardless of the player's Phase 38 display name. FIX: playerName() now returns G.settings.mpName || "Rayan" (the Phase 38 setting, per-slot through the settings record), and the dead literal no longer exists. (b) THE SOVEREIGN COULD BE SILENTLY LOST: with a full garage, crown4's delivery files the Sovereign ONLY as the harborfall-garage-car snapshot — and the villa/apartment drive auto-save overwrites that snapshot with any car parked in the zone for >2 s in the SAME session, before any reload could restore the Sovereign into the world fleet; crownCarDelivered then blocked re-delivery forever. FIX: crown.ts gains reconcileSovereign() — if M.crownDone ∧ M.crownCarDelivered ∧ no Sovereign exists in storedCars, the garage snapshot or the world fleet, it clears the flag and re-runs deliverSovereign(); App.tsx calls it once on mount (poster-load effect). Verified headlessly 8/8 (re-delivery into storedCars when the snapshot was overwritten; silence when the car exists in the fleet; the crownDone gate; full-garage re-file; the re-filed snapshot visible to sovereignExists; the duplicate guard). The general snapshot-overwrite behaviour is BY DESIGN (the slot is the single "car at the villa" slot, overwritten routinely) — only the one-time reward made an overwrite a permanent loss, which is why the fix is scoped to the Sovereign. B.2 STAND-DOWN CENSUS (activity × interruption, read from the code): NG+ is comprehensive in ngplus.ts — mission, escort, dispatch, rental, race, Slide, taxi/delivery/tow/farm/courier/valet phases, service shifts, snag (endSnagRun), derby (unsettled), regatta (unsettled), fishing cast, tram/bus, eventNow, hold, wanted, heat. BUST/WASTED run through G.status-edge watchers: snag (Phase7), service shifts (ServiceJobs), regatta (DayEvents), Slide (SlideSeries) stand down explicitly; the legacy jobs self-heal by their own long-standing rules — taxi abandons after >4 s out of the cab, valet has its 5 s out-of-car rule and collect, courier times out, races abandon on leaving the car, the marina rental is day-scoped and self-collects at the day roll, the derby is a timed window that settles at the whistle, the escort cancels on G.status. DAY TURN: the snag self-ends on its serial stamp, day-event records go inert (serial-keyed), a live regatta run stands down. SLOT SWITCH reloads the page (switchSlot), so all session state dies by construction and per-slot keys isolate the saves. No leaking cell found. One engine-wide behaviour noted, not changed: mission timers keep running during the bust/wasted overlay — consistent for every mission since long before Phase 26. B.3 SINGLE-SOURCE CENSUS — CLEAN: businessDailyNet (Bank rows, Bank total and the tick; events + club term + Crown dividend all inside it); rentDailyNet/rentFor (Bank = tick; tenant events are documented adjustments ON TOP, each messaged with its exact amount at fire time — they are events, not a second rent computation); marketPrice (ModMenu's display dealCost and buy() both price through it, and buy() re-reads it live per the Phase 48 fix); sellStash (prices off the one stashTotal and consumes the inventory atomically in the same synchronous call — the market's +20% is a bonusFrac argument, not a second price); the drift feed reads rawSteering at the bank() call site while the car steers on the assisted value (Phase 44's construction). Greps for rogue ×1.1 / 0.75 / income recomputations found only unrelated animation and physics constants. B.4 MONEY EXACTLY-ONCE (26–48 paths) — CLEAN: the inspection fine lives inside rollBizEvents under advanceDay's serial guard plus a per-business serial check, clamps to cash on hand and persists synchronously; the regatta purse sets DAY.regattaPrizePaid and persists BEFORE the cash lands, with REG.active cleared on finish's first line; postcard fees set + persist the done flag BEFORE the cash in completePostcard; the fixer bribe passes App's refusal chain (incl. the ECO.daySerial ≤ HEAT.fixerDay cooldown) and applyBribe charges once and persists; the crown escrow is Part A (1). Double-click, double-frame and reload-mid-path were traced for each — every path is guarded by a persisted flag flipped in the same synchronous handler as the money. One adjacent pattern noted, not changed: mission cash persists at completion while side progress advances in the outro dialogue callback — the engine-wide pattern since Phase 5 (story progress identical); a reload mid-outro re-offers the mission. Pre-existing, not a 26–48 regression. B.5 C++ SMOKE + PARITY: g++ -O2 -std=c++17 (physics.cpp + smoke_test.cpp) — 49/49 PASS, "ALL PASS" (the Phase 25 depenetration cases included). No parity script ships in the repo — parity is by construction (exact mirrors) — so a fresh probe was run for this round: the TS fallbackPhysics (esbuild-bundled) against the compiled C++ over building_overlap, resolve_overlap, step_vehicle_v3 and swim_step — 20 probe lines, worst absolute difference 4.441e-16 (floating-point associativity only). THE STANDING LINE HOLDS: client/public carries no deployed.wasm in this tree; the loader falls back per function to the TS mirrors; the WASM binary is not rebuilt in this environment (no Emscripten) — source and mirrors are what is verified. ONE DEFECT FOUND HERE: scripts/build-physics.sh's EXPORTED_FUNCTIONS had drifted behind GamePhysics.ts — _building_overlap, _resolve_overlap and _ragdoll_step were cwrap'd by the loader but missing from the export list, so a future emcc rebuild would still never have activated the WASM path for depenetration or ragdoll (today's runtime is safe: each cwrap sits in its own try/catch over the TS mirrors). FIX: the three names added; all three verified present inside physics.cpp's extern "C" block. B.6 SERVER: pytest 48/48. Bounds side by side — server (server/main.py): WORLD X −244..178, Z −416..372. Client: boat chart clamp x [−240, 170], z [−412, 368] (Vehicle.tsx); swim |x| < 175, z −388..365 (Player.tsx); streamed land extents Eastmere x ≤ 122, Westbrook x ≥ −196, Northreach z ≥ −368 (world.ts). Every client-reachable position sits strictly inside the server bounds. (The boat's east clamp is an 8u inset rather than 4u — inside bounds either way; noted, not a violation.) B.7 BUILD/CHUNKS: initial load 641.18 kB gzip (index 265.51 + three 178.23 + react-three 185.77 + CSS 11.01 + HTML 0.66) — the Phase 48 class, +0.03 kB for this round's fixes. Lazy and confirmed lazy: posterData 236.14, cc0Data 959.18, Phone 18.29, Eastmere 3.20, Westbrook 2.94, Northreach 2.94, ModMenu 1.78, GaragePanel 1.75, CharacterSelect 0.73 kB gzip. Placement audit: ALL PLACEMENTS CLEAR (all 14 sections). B.8 DOCS: README carries a per-phase entry through Phase 48 and gains the Phase 49 bullet this round; no unbacked README claim found in the checked surface; STORY.md covers every arc including the Harbour Crown. VISUAL_AUDIT gains its Phase 49 section (audit round — nothing visual claimed). *Phase 50 note (v3.0 finale — tally RECOUNTED: 87/7/0 over 94 lines → **104 ✅ / 8 🟡 / 0 ⬜ over 112 lines**; no line changed state). PART A — PHASE 49 FIX VERIFICATION. (1) LEADERBOARD NAME: playerName() in game/leaderboard.ts returns G.settings.mpName || "Rayan" (the Phase 38 Settings field — the same string the WS join sends), and it is the ONLY name source for service posts: postRaceResult (leaderboards) and postEconomy (economy snapshot) both build their bodies from it, and every caller (Phase7 race finishes, Phase8's daily snapshot, DayEvents' regatta settle) goes through those two functions — no other post in the client constructs a player name. The dead "harborfall-mp-name" string survives only inside the comment that documents the old bug; it is never read or written. (2) SOVEREIGN RECONCILE: reconcileSovereign() is called once, in App's mount effect (empty dependency list), and returns immediately unless M.crownDone ∧ M.crownCarDelivered ∧ no Sovereign exists anywhere; deliverSovereign() re-checks the same guards before filing, so the pair cannot loop, double-file or mint a duplicate. It cannot fire during a live crown mission: the call happens at app mount, when no mission is active (mission state is session state; a reload mid-mission restarts at the checkpoint machinery, not inside a live mission block), and the function touches only the garage filing — never mission state. (3) WASM EXPORT LIST: diffed mechanically — GamePhysics.ts cwrap names (23) vs scripts/build-physics.sh EXPORTED_FUNCTIONS minus _malloc/_free (23): EXACT MATCH, including the Phase 49 additions _building_overlap / _resolve_overlap / _ragdoll_step. (4) NG+ CENSUS COMMENT — ONE GAP FOUND AND FIXED: the Phase 49 census comment in ngplus.ts decided most keys by name, but three keys the code keeps were never named: harborfall-stash (fenceable inventory), harborfall-wpmods (owned weapon mods), harborfall-throw (throwables). The code's behaviour was already the deliberate one (NG+ never touches them — the reset restarts the story, not the player's pockets); the comment now names all three, so the census is complete as written. PART B — THE FINALE. VERSION: 3.0.0 on client/package.json and its lockfile root entries (the lock had drifted at 1.0.0 against the package's 2.0.0 — both now read 3.0.0), desktop/package.json (was 1.0.0), server/main.py's FastAPI version (was 2.0.0), and the start-screen stamp, which now reads "v3.0 — Complete Edition" in the same .version-stamp style.
